みずほ経済 EXPRESS

対日直接投資拡大で国内供給力の強化へ 
～戦略 17 分野でみた外資誘致の現状と課題～

調査部  エコノミスト 阿部大樹 
080-3709-6077 taiki.abe@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 高まる国内投資の機運。 国内供給力強化に向けては対内直接投資の取り込みも重要に 
政府は2026年7月、 日本成長戦略会議において、 国内供給力の強化に向けた官民投資ロードマップを
決定した。とりわけ経済安全保障上の重要性や成長期待が高い17の戦略分野においては、2040年度ま
でに累計370兆円超の官民投資が想定されている（図表1）。日本政策投資銀行「2026年度設備投資計
画調査」では、大企業の約58％、中堅企業の約43％が戦略分野における国内投資を強化すると答えて
おり、多くの企業が政府の方針を前向きに受け止めていることがうかがえる。 
国内投資促進の主軸を担うのは政府や国内民間企業だが、国内供給力を強化していくうえでは、外
国資本の取り込み、すなわち対内直接投資の拡大を促すことも重要だ。対内直接投資とは、外国企業
が日本企業への出資や融資を通じて、日本国内の企業経営・事業に継続的に関わることを目的とした
投資のことをいう。政府も対日直接投資推進会議で、戦略分野ごとに有望な外国企業を発掘し誘致す
る方針を掲げている。 
対内直接投資は、 投資形態の違いから四つに分類できる （図表2） 。 このうち、 「合併・買収」 や 「企

### Page 2

2

業・財務再編」は国内供給力の強化につながらない可能性もあるが、「グリーンフィールド投資」と
「能力増強投資」は工場・生産ラインなど生産資本の増加をともない、供給網のレジリエンス向上や
国内の供給力強化につながりやすい形態だ。本稿では、これら二つの形態を合わせて「国内供給力強
化投資」と呼ぶこととする。以下では「国内供給力強化投資」に焦点を絞り、戦略分野ごとに直近5年
間の投資流入状況を確認したうえで、対内直接投資の今後の増加余地と誘致のあり方を検討する。 
■ AI・データセンター関連投資等のグローバルな機運が国内供給力強化投資をけん引  
日本の対内直接投資は、2010年代半ば以降、拡大傾向にある。国際収支統計で集計される対内直接
投資の動向を示した図表3をみると、 その力強い伸びが顕著に見て取れる。GDP比でも1.0％ 程 度（2025
年）まで増加しており、外国企業の国内投資志向が高まりつつある証左と言えよう。 
先行きは、さらなる対内直接投資の拡大が期待できそうだ。予定段階の個別投資案件を集計してい
るfDi Marketsのデータで国内供給力強化投資の動向を確認すると、2020年代に入って投資金額が大幅
に拡大していることがわかる （図表4）。同データでみられる投資拡大の動きは、クロスボーダー金融
取引の実績を計上している国際収支統計でも今後顕在化してくるとみられる。 
さらに、個別投資案件のデータを確認することで、国際収支統計では把握できなかった対内直接投
資の性質・内容を詳細に分析することが可能である。戦略分野別に国内供給力強化投資をみると、AI・
半導体分野や、データセンター関連が含まれるデジタル・サイバーセキュリティ分野で投資金額が大
きく、 かつ大幅な伸びが確認できる （図表5） 。 世界的なAIブームを受けたデータセンター関連投資の
活発化が背景とみられる。半導体製造設備やデータセンター関連の投資には巨額の資本が必要になる
ため、投資金額を件数で割った一件あたりの投資額が大規模化しやすく、大型投資案件が投資額全体
を押し上げていることがうかがえる。 
次に投資金額が大きかったのは資源・エネルギー安全保障・GX分野だ。具体的には、発電所などの
電力供給設備への投資案件が押し上げている。太陽光発電・洋上風力発電などの再生可能エネルギー
市場で海外企業の参入が増えた結果とみられ、脱炭素化を志向するグローバルな機運が追い風になっ
図表3 対内直接投資 図表4 国内供給力強化投資

ていると言える。加えて、電力需要の大きいデータセンターの立地を最適化するため、電力源と通信
インフラを一体的に整備する「ワットビット連携」が推進される中で、電力供給設備への投資需要が
高まっていることも当該分野の投資押上げに寄与しているとみられる。 
これらの分野では引き続きグローバルトレンドに沿った投資流入増が見込めそうである一方、造船
分野や防衛産業分野、量子分野、海洋分野の投資額は、増加しているものの依然として小規模だ。例
えば、量子分野は研究開発～実証段階の技術が中心の新領域であり、分野の将来的な成長期待は大き
い。現時点では収益化まで距離があることから、投資額の小ささ自体が問題とは言えないものの、米
国等と比べると日本の産業基盤は相対的に小さく、 スタートアップ企業の集積でも遅れを取っている。
さらに投資を呼び込むうえでは、こうした遅れを埋める手立てを講じることが必要不可欠であろう。 
また、造船分野では、2020年代に入ってから中国の存在感が一段と高まっている。日本の造船会社
による船舶の新規受注量は2000年代半ばから減少しており、世界の船舶建造量に占める日本のシェア
も低下傾向だ。背景には、中国と比べた事業所規模の小ささが指摘されており、生産コストなどの面
で水をあけられている。 
■ 幅広い分野での外国資本取り込みに向けて供給面 ・ 制度面のボトルネック軽減が重要 
戦略分野全体でみれば投資流入の堅調さが確認できた一方で、17の戦略分野それぞれにおいて投資
流入増を実現していく1ためには、 投資の門戸を開くだけでは不十分だ。 造船分野や量子分野に関して
指摘したような課題の解決に向けては、ボトルネックを軽減する地道な取り組みが必要であろう。ま
た、足元で急速に拡大しているデータセンター投資や再エネ関連投資についても、今後は成長の維持
が課題となる。 
外国資本取り込みにおける具体的なボトルネックとして、人材不足等の供給制約や複雑な制度、言
語障壁が挙げられる。日本国内の外資系企業を対象にした、日本貿易振興機構による「2025年度外資
系企業ビジネス実態調査」 では、 直近1～2年の日本のビジネス環境の課題について、 多くの企業が 「高
度人材等の確保」 と 「一般人材の確保」 を回答しているほか、 直近では 「オフィス・事業用地の確保」
図表5 戦略17分野別にみた国内供給力強化投資額の推移

（注） 各社資料で確認できなかった具体的な投資額はfDi Marketsによる推計値。 期間内に国内供給力強化投資が確認できなかった分野につい
ては除外。各社の個別投資プロジェクトの情報及び、日本成長戦略会議資料を基に、みずほ総合研究所調査部が分類

### Page 4

4

を回答する企業の割合も高まっている。こうした人材・立地面等の供給制約は、外資の取り込みを進
めるうえで課題となる。 
また、制度面について、日本貿易振興機構による「ジェトロ対日投資報告2018」では、外国企業が
日本でビジネス展開するうえでの阻害要因として「行政手続きの複雑さ」や「許認可制度の厳しさ」
等が挙げられ、外国企業にとって日本のビジネス環境への適応が容易ではないことがうかがえる。な
お、 同調査では 「外国語によるコミュニケーションの難しさ」 が2位にランクするなど、 言語障壁も外
国企業の参入を阻む一因だ。 
供給面・制度面のボトルネック解消に向けた対応として、人材不足については、大学等の研究・教
育機関との連携強化を通じた国内人材の育成・確保や、高度外国人材の受入強化による対応が考えら
れる。また、用地不足に対しては、産業用地の確保や空き家・空き地の有効活用を通じて、外国企業
が拠点を設立しやすい環境を整備することが重要だろう。 
制度面の対応としては、まず、手続きの一本化が挙げられる。現状、外国企業等の投資申請に際し
ては担当省庁・公的機関と個別にやり取りするケースがあり、外国企業の手続き負担は小さいとは言
えない。 この点、OECD（2026） は外国企業 ・ 投資家と関係省庁等との調整を担う 「ワンストップ窓口」
の機能強化を提言している。 言語障壁については近年通訳や英訳による手続き支援が進みつつあるが、
主要な政策等については英語アナウンスを適時提供するなど、言語障壁軽減に向けたさらなる取り組
みが求められる。

［参考文献］ 
OECD (2022), FDI Qualities Policy Toolkit, OECD Publishing 
OECD (2025), OECD Benchmark Definition of Foreign Direct Investment,  5th edition, OECD 
Publishing 
OECD (2026), OECD Economic Surveys: Japan 2026, OECD Publishing

1 コンテンツ産業のような日本企業に競争力がある分野では、外国資本の取り込みによる事業拡大も期待される一方、独自技術
や知的財産の流出リスクには留意し、慎重に議論を進める必要がある。

身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
中東緊張暫緩，風險資產全面反攻
2026 年 5 月
4 月全球主要股市報酬全面轉正，與 3 月跌勢形成鮮明對比，AI 題材再度成為主軸，重燃市場熱
度，風險資產動能強勁回溫。以美元計價 4 月表現觀察，新興亞洲與新興市場上漲分別上漲 17.4%
與 14.5%，表現最為亮麗，主要受台灣與南韓在 AI 半導體供應鏈帶動下的強勁買盤支撐；標普 500
的 4 月漲幅也創 2020 年 11 月以來最佳月表現 。 然而 ， 拉丁美洲與東協市因與科技題材連結度較低，
使其期間漲幅相對緩和。不過，MSCI 拉丁美洲指數今年前 4 個月的漲幅仍高達 17.3%，為所有主要

股市之冠。
隨著市場風險接受度提高，全球主要債市4 月也同步反彈。新興當地債券單月上漲2.79%表現最
佳，主要因美元指數當月由約 100 降至近 98，使新興當地債換算回美元後的價格水漲船高。波動較
大的亞洲高收益債、新興美元債券、全球高收益債表現也有所表現。全球投資級公司債、全球主權債
券等債券雖亦分別上漲 1.36%、1.25%，但幅度上相對落後。
4 月市場大幅反彈的核心驅動力來自三方面：其一，投資人對中東戰事可能導致通膨上揚的疑慮
明顯鈍化；其二，AI 主軸再度成為主導行情的關鍵主題，包括 Alphabet 雲端與 AI 業務需求暢旺帶
動半導體類股大漲；其三，總經基本面韌性意外優於預期，整體勞動市場仍穩定。然而，霍爾木茲海
峽運輸尚未完全恢復，中東戰事變數仍存，地緣風險仍是市場最大不確定性來源。
根據彭博 5/4 的統計，今年 Q1 與 Q2 EPS 年增率均可望突破兩成，我們預期 2026 年標普 500
企業獲利增長動能持續 ，AI 仍是核心驅動主軸。儘管短期股市可能因中東戰事帶來通膨隱憂而波動，

但我們認為企業基本面強度足以抵銷中東衝突與政策不確定性 ，而企業強勁的獲利成長將可望為全球
股市中期走勢帶來支撐。
債市方面，受到美伊戰爭影響，4 月的 FOMC 會議會後聲明偏鷹，壓抑市場降息預期，考量後續
美伊和談時程仍有不確定性 ，預期長天期公債殖利率波動風險將加劇 。 由於目前美國中短天期公債殖
利率顯著高於過去 10 年均值，且中短期債券到期期間較短，具備資金靈活運用彈性，因此建議在債
券天期選擇上 ， 以中短天期美國公債及高評級投資級債為主 ， 並以平衡投資組合波動角度做適當配置 。

美國股市：美股盈餘增長強勁，AI 主題重獲市場青睞
4 月美股雖受伊朗戰爭不確定性干擾，在財報優異及 AI 產業高速成長帶動下，標普 500 指數續
創歷史新高。指數上揚主要由輝達、Alphabet、亞馬遜、博通、蘋果、微軟、超微、英特爾、美光
等科技股帶動，表現相對弱勢的族群包括埃克森美孚、雪佛龍、嬌生、默克藥廠、亞培、ServiceNow、
AT&T、洛克希德馬丁、雷神等，市場焦點已經從能源、國防回歸 AI 主題。
美國通膨墊高，但經濟表現仍具韌性，支持聯準會更長時間維持利率。4 月 ISM 製造業 PMI 持
平於 52.7，連續第四個月維持擴張區間。其中供應商交貨指數從 58.9 升至 60.6，反映伊朗戰爭與航

運通道風險升高，導致供應鏈壓力再度升溫；客戶庫存指數降至 39.1，持續偏低；新訂單指數小幅
上升至 54.1，訂單動能尚具支撐，積壓訂單由 54.4 大幅降至 51.4，新出口訂單進一步下滑至 47.9，
顯示外需偏弱；價格指數從 78.3 攀升至 84.6；就業指數降至 46.4，顯示企業尚未恢復積極擴編意願。
故製造業雖維持擴張 ， 但由於庫存已經偏低 ，若戰事持續恐將加速推升通膨。4 月份 ISM 服務業 PMI
指數由前月的 54.0 下降至 53.6，低於市場預期的 53.7，新訂單指數由前月的 60.6 下降至 53.5，低
於市場預期的 57.3，支付價格指數持平於 70.7，物價雖仍在高檔但並未惡化。同時預期新任聯準會
主席 Warsh 上台後，要等到現任主席鮑威爾完全辭去 Fed 理事職務，才會有較大的空間與明確度去
推進「相信 AI 將帶來生產力提振、進而支持降息」的政策論述，故降息時間或許延後，未來利率將
更長時間維持不變。
AI 帶動科技股財報表現優異。預估第一季標普 500 企業盈餘年增可達到 23.9%，且第二季盈餘

增長仍維持近兩成水準，科技板塊受惠於 AI，仍是帶動美股企業獲利增長主軸，但到了第二季，能
源價格維持高檔下將推升能源產業獲利，且若伊朗戰事無法落幕，預料各種原物料價格都將受影響走
高，標普 500 企業獲利成長性並不看淡。
美國金融股第一季財報普遍優於預期，對消費惡化與私募基金流動性的擔憂已經淡化。由於股票
與商品市場交投熱絡，帶動承銷與交易收入顯著成長，各大銀行放款規模持續擴張，淨利息收入成長
優於預期。消費信用方面，信用卡支出與消費貸款餘額仍具韌性，整體資產品質良好。
目前標普 500 在指數續創新高下本益比來到 26 倍、股價淨值比 5.36 倍，短期評價偏高，且通
膨風險逐漸增加、伊朗戰爭的不確定性仍在，雖美國科技龍頭在 AI 產業具備領先優勢，但短期需留
意市場過熱及估值偏高風險，建議以長線角度分批布局。

歐洲股市：歐洲央行態度謹慎，川普加徵歐洲汽車關稅
4 月歐股受能源價格大漲衝擊，雖財報優異但科技股比重相對美股較低，泛歐 600 指數緩步墊高。
指數上揚主要由艾司摩爾、西門子、西門子能源、匯豐、艾波比、英飛凌、施耐德電機、諾基亞、瑞
銀集團、諾和諾德等企業帶動，表現相對弱勢的族群包括殼牌、德意志電信、阿斯特捷利康、諾華、
葛蘭素史克、聯合利華、依視路羅薩奧蒂卡、赫力昂、挪威國家石油、BP 等，市場焦點已經從能源、
國防題材回歸科技與金融股。
4 月歐元區 服務業受到物價飆漲與能源支出暴增的雙重打擊， 服務 業採購經理人指數 急挫至

47.4，創疫情後最差表現；然而製造業採購經理人指數則在新訂單改善(尤其法國)及避險備貨需求的
支撐下，逆勢攀升至 52.2，然而就業人數持續萎縮，反映企業在高通膨與需求不確定性下普遍採取
縮編策略，經濟放緩格局明顯。法國製造業 PMI 攀升至 52.8，主要得益於新訂單走高，反映需求復
甦；德國製造業 PMI 雖達 51.4，增長動能依賴企業拉升庫存，能否延續仍須觀察。
儘管歐洲央行在 4 月利率決策會議按兵不動，但此舉是反映需要更多時間凝聚升息共識，並蒐集
能源衝擊對通膨的實際影響數據 。歐洲央行總裁透露委員會內部曾認真討論升息的可能性，維持不動
的理由包括：近期數據仍接近3 月預測、貨幣政策在能源價格上漲前已處於適當位置、金融與信貸條
件已開始收緊，目前局勢「偏離」原先基準情境，通膨上行風險與經濟成長下行風險均在加劇，但歐
洲經濟尚未進入停滯。市場預期 ECB 將於 2026 年升息 2 碼，甚至不排除於年底或於明年升息第 3
碼，但考量歐洲通膨仍與伊朗戰事息息相關，若能源價格回落、需求走弱，則升息時點可能延後；若

能源價格繼續在高檔盤旋、通膨持續性跡象提早浮現，不排除6 月即會啟動第一次升息。英格蘭銀行
(英國央行)在最新利率決策會議中維持利率不變，但整體立場偏向謹慎，市場同樣預期未來半年可能
有 2 碼升息幅度。近期 ECB 與 BOE 的升息預期已帶動歐元、英鎊轉強。
川普於 5/1 宣布，由於歐盟不遵守貿易協議，將對歐盟進口汽車與卡車加徵 25%關稅(不包含英
國)。由於歐洲議會在 3/26 已有條件通過歐美貿易協議的相關立法，故川普調整汽車業關稅從 15%
到 25%，可能引發美歐貿易戰再次啟動，接下來預料歐盟祭出關稅反制措施且展開新一輪談判。
泛歐 600 指數目前本益比為 16.87，股價淨值比為 2.39，目前面臨能源價格仍在高檔、川普對歐
盟汽車業加徵關稅，短期風險增加。然而考量評價合理、伊朗戰爭持續降溫且預料美歐經過談判，終
將能達成貿易協議，歐股仍可以長線角度、作為分批佈局標的。

日本股市：日本利率按兵不動，AI 題材帶動指數創高
4 月上旬日股受地緣政治風險衝擊，所幸在 AI 題材帶動下科技股走高，日經指數更於 4 月一度
收盤站上 60,537 點，以收盤價站上 6 萬點歷史新高。市場主要由軟銀、愛德萬測試、Keyence、村
田製作所、三菱電機、三菱 UFJ 金融、東京威力科創、藤倉、鎧俠、三井住友金融等公司帶動，表
現相對弱勢的族群包括三菱商事、豐田汽車、任天堂、三井物產、永旺、武田藥品、國際石油開發帝
石、7&I、山之內製藥、東方樂園等，市場焦點回歸 AI、國防，以及升息可能帶動的金融股。

相較於歐洲汽車出口至美國關稅再度被川普調高，目前並未傳出美國可能調高日本進口汽車關稅
的訊息，顯示高市早苗先前與川普會晤帶來實質外交成效。日本進入黃金週，但高市首相選擇飛往越
南與澳洲「拼外交」，尤其將澳洲定位為「準同盟」 ，推動以「最上級」護衛艦為基礎的共同開發與輸
出，顯然高市對於國防自主有堅定決心。
日本央行 4 月利率決策會議以 6 比 3 同意將利率維持於 0.75%，三位委員認為應將政策利率調
高至 1.0%，市場對後續升息的預期明顯升溫。日本央行同時將 FY2026 核心 CPI 預測大幅上調 0.9
個百分點至+2.8%，FY2027 上調 0.3 個百分點至+2.3%，這些預測建立在油價將從每桶約 105 美元
回落至 70～80 美元的假設之上 ， 故若油價長期維持高位 ， 通膨前景可能在7 月展望報告中再度上修 ，
而對 GDP 增長預期也小幅下修。對比會後聲明透露出來的鷹派立場，總裁植田和男會後記者會發言
立場則略顯保守，持續淡化央行升息時點，強調需要時間觀察經濟及通膨發展，且提及達成央行經濟

預估的前景下降 ， 尚無法確定需要多久來評估下次升息時點 。 鑒於過於弱勢的日圓匯率可能更進一步
推高國內通膨，日本央行仍有高機率於第三季上調政策利率，若油價再度上行，甚至不排除在 6 月就
採取行動。
截至 4 月 24 日當周，外資買超日股 8,079 億日圓，2026 年以來外資對日股的累計買超金額來
到 7.97 兆日圓，改寫今年以來累計加碼金額紀錄。近期日財長與外匯最高官員相繼釋出干預匯市警
告、加上外資持續匯入且市場對 BOJ 升息的預期，日圓貶值趨勢已扭轉。近期日圓一度單日升值 3%，
顯示 160 為日本政府當局匯價的干預底線，預料近期日圓仍將於 155 至 158 附近震盪。
2026 年以來日股 TOPIX 指數受惠於 AI 題材、高市擴大財政刺激、強化軍事安全，但本益比仍
在 19.39 倍，股價淨值比在 1.77 倍。近期日圓守住 160 關卡，匯率穩定可降低投資者不確定性，預
料日本企業股票回購仍持續推進，可望為日股在全球金融市場震盪下帶來支撐。

台灣股市：強勁經濟與產業基本面支撐下的長多格局
4 月台灣股市在 AI 超級週期與全球資金的簇擁下 ， 正式跨越了四萬點 ， 本次台股的突破並非單純
的資金行情，而是具備了極為堅實的總體經濟、強健的產業鏈獲利基本面，以及法規鬆綁帶來的籌碼
結構重塑。
台灣今年初展現了令全球市場矚目的經濟爆發力。主計總處公布的第一季 GDP 成長率概估高達
13.69%，創下近 39 年來的單季最高紀錄。這股強大動能的源頭，清晰地反映在經濟部的三月份外銷
訂單數據上， 三月單月接單金額飆升至 911.2 億美元 ， 年增率高達65.9%， 連續14 個月維持正成長。

資訊通信與電子產品年增率分別高達 120.9%與 73.7%，這顯示台灣在全球 AI 基礎設施、高效能運
算（HPC）以及雲端資料中心建設中，扮演著不可替代的樞紐角色。亮眼的總經數據，為台股的高檔
估值提供了最強而有力的下檔支撐。
中經院 4 月製造業 PMI 大幅跳升 4.9 個百分點至 60.3%，連續 7 個月呈現擴張，更是近 55 個月
以來最快的擴張速度。除了 AI 與半導體的實質需求外，中東地緣政治的緊張局勢促使製造業啟動了
「防禦性提前拉貨」 。短期內這將維持製造業的熱絡，但也必須持續監控輸入性通膨的潛在風險。
宏觀數據之外，台積電於 4 月中旬召開的法說會，無疑是穩住外資與台股軍心的定海神針。財報
顯示，第一季 EPS 達 22.08 元，毛利率以 66.2%飛越財測高標。其中高效能運算（HPC）相關營收
佔比已正式突破六成 （達 61%） ，並上修了2026 全年美元營收成長目標至 30% 以上。這場法說會
不僅確認了台積電高階製程的護城河，也確立了全球 AI 伺服器供應鏈的長線能見度。

在基本面一片大好之際，金管會放寬了主動型基金與主動式 ETF 對單一個股 10%的持股上限，
從 10%放寬至 25%。這項法規鬆綁產生了極為強大的「流動性溢價」效應。過去受限於10%緊箍咒
的機構、 基金資金 ， 如今得以大舉回補台積電 、 聯發科等核心權值股 。這讓大盤指數在衝破四萬點後，
資金高度向權值龍頭集中，成為加權指數的進一步推收力量。
綜合亮眼的總經數據、良好的企業基本面，以及籌碼向大型股集中的法規紅利，我們對台股整體
的長線投資建議維持加碼。然而，目前加權指數與季線的正乖離率已來到 18%的歷史相對高位，意
味著市場隨時可能出現技術性拉回與獲利了結的賣壓。必須強調的是，牛市中的急跌與拉回屬於 「健
康的籌碼換手」 ，並非長線趨勢的反轉。面對當前乖離頗大、但長線看多的盤勢，建議投資人不追高，
待拉回後布局，預留部分現金，保留流動性以迎接拉回時的絕佳買點。

科技類股：半導體產業持續看好，分批佈局雲端巨頭
回顧 4 月份，在地緣政治影響逐漸淡化之後，華爾街對 AI 硬體的「錯失恐懼症（FOMO） 」全面
爆發。費城半導體指數上演了史無前例的連續 18 個交易日上漲，單月狂飆近 40%。這波資金浪潮不
僅將指數推向歷史新高。支撐這波半導體狂飆的是極其扎實的財報利多，不僅英特爾靠著晶圓代工與
CPU 獲利超預期迎來大復活，記憶體族群更步入了史無前例的「超級循環」 。由於AI 伺服器對 HBM
與高階 SSD 需求爆發，導致記憶體陷入結構性大缺貨。美光（Micron）與威騰（WDC）等大廠握

有絕對的定價權，這再次確認了半導體作為「AI 算力基礎設施」的長線多頭地位。
在剛公布的財報中，四大雲端巨頭交出了令人驚豔的成績單。微軟的 AI 相關年度經常性收入大
增，Google Cloud 也寫下逾 60%的驚人成長，Meta 則透過 AI 優化廣告投放，拉升利潤率，亞馬
遜（AWS）的營收增速也創下近期新高。這些數據證明，掌握龐大生態系的巨頭，已經能將 AI 算力
成功轉化為自身業務的現金流。此外，根據 2026 Q1 財報數據，四大雲端服務供應商並未因高基期
而放慢腳步，反而持續加大投入，全年資本支出（Capex）預計由原先估計的 6,000 億美元進一步增
加至近 7,000 億美元。在這波驚人的支出中除了晶片設計商外，代工的台積電、供應HBM 的三星與
海力士，以及伺服器組裝與散熱解決方案的廠商都因此受惠，這也是硬體續漲的主要原因。
然而儘管 CSP 業者的支出續增，但投資人顯然不再全面買單，市場進入「AI 變現檢驗期」 。由於
龐大的折舊壓力與變現的陣痛 ，四大 CSP 業者在財報後的股價表現各不相同 。Google 與亞馬遜成功

用現有核心業務的強勁現金流護航 AI 支出，成為資金的避風港；反觀微軟與 Meta，因資本支出預
期大幅暴增 ， 市場擔憂長期支出深不見底 ，變現遙遙無期 ，積極的佈局反而成為短期壓抑股價的包袱 。
然而四大 CSP 的 EPS 年增率依然維持在雙位數，但評價面卻因資本開支焦慮被壓低，下檔風險相對
受控，這提供了一個逢低布局的機會。
AI 產業的長多格局不變，但在進入 5 月後，市場可能面臨傳統的「Sell in M ay」季節性逆風，
且 2026 年適逢美國期中選舉，歷史經驗顯示第二、三季往往是波動最劇烈的時期。因此，我們建議
半導體類股仍是不可或缺的持股，但也應該開始逢低布局美股七巨頭等大型科技類股。 七巨頭的預估
本益比已經回落至合理區間，且具備穩健的現金流。加上隨著 Google 的 TPU、亞馬遜的 Trainium
等自研晶片部署比例提升，CSP 業者將逐步降低對高價 GPU 的依賴，這將顯著降低長期推論成本。
因此建議適時布局相關類股，來迎接下半年變現能力轉強後的估值修復行情。

AI 電力類股：資料中心用電需求倍增，有利電力基礎建設發展
IEA(國際能源署)發布 2026 年 Key Questions on Energy and AI 報告，指出受到 AI 快速發展影
響，全球用電需求將急遽成長，有利於相關電力基礎建設發展。IEA 指出，全球資料中心用電量在 2024
至 2025 年間成長逾 15%，年增約 70 太瓦時至 485 太瓦時，占全球電力需求略超過 1.5%，未來幾
年用電需求更將進一步加速，IEA 預估全球資料中心用電將從 2024 年的 415 太瓦時倍增至 2030 年
的 950 太瓦時，屆時將占全球電力需求的 3%，AI 專用資料中心用電量更將成長三倍至 465 太瓦時。

驅動電力需求暴增的核心因素是 AI 伺服器功耗密度的指數級攀升。以 Nvidia 為例，2020 年
Ampere 架構機架功耗密度約 13 千瓦時，當前 Blackwell 架構已達 130 千瓦時，下一代 Rubin 架構
將躍升至 600 千瓦時， 屆時單一機架尖峰用電量將等同約 65 戶家庭 。在 AI 伺服器功耗上升的同時，
大型科技巨頭資本支出也持續攀升，為 AI 伺服器帶來更多需求，2025 年科技巨頭資本支出合計已超
過 4,000 億美元，2026 年預計再增 75%至 7,150 億美元，IEA 更進一步預估 2026 至 2030 年間資
料中心累計投資將達 3.9 兆美元，隨資料中心大量建置，AI 用電需求將隨之攀升。
此外，IEA 指出儘管單一 AI 任務的能耗效率正以前所未見的速度改善，單一任務功耗每年約可
降至十分之一，然而新興高耗能應用如影片生成、推理及代理式 AI 也持續推陳出新，這些新功能的
能耗可達到簡單文字生成的數百至數千倍 ，導致即便 AI 效率持續提升，整體能耗需求仍然有增無減。

在 AI 用電需求大增的同時，目前電力基礎建設供給端各環節均出現嚴重瓶頸，相關關鍵零組件
因產能不足，導致交期持續延長，包含變壓器交期平均需二至三年，燃氣輪機交期長達五年，電網併
聯在美國多數地區也須排隊長達五至十年。以德州 ERCOT 獨立電網為例，資料中心併網排隊量從
2024 年 12 月的 63 吉瓦時暴增至 2026 年 1 月逾 230 吉瓦時，但該州歷史尖峰負載僅 85 吉瓦時，
顯示電力需求遠超既有電網承載量。可預見隨電力需求持續攀升，相關電力基礎建設上中下游未來訂
單積壓量都可望保持高水位，並隨公司產能陸續開出，逐步轉換為公司營收貢獻。
展望後市，IEA 預估全球資料中心發電量至 2030 年將倍增至超過 1,000 太瓦時，其中再生能源
與天然氣各占約三分之一，分別達 360 及 340 太瓦時，核能預料將從 75 太瓦時成長至 120 太瓦時，
故各類相關零組件包含燃氣輪機、變壓器、核能及儲能等子產業都可望受惠這一 AI 用電趨勢，隨 AI
電力需求持續上升，對於各類電力上中下游供應鏈都可正向看待。

能源類股：海峽受阻導致油價居高不下；阿聯酋退出 OPEC 有利戰後供給恢復
西德州原油期貨價格 4 月再度攀升至每桶 100 美元之上，主因經荷莫茲海峽的石油出口持續受
阻，加上美伊談判持續未見明顯進展，同時期間內美國總統川普已數次拒絕伊朗所提出之協議，美國
對伊朗的封鎖行動也持續進行，故在全球石油供需缺口維持的狀況下，全球石油庫存水位不斷下降，
為石油價格帶來上行壓力。
國際能源署 4 月月報指出，受荷莫茲海峽供應中斷影響，3 月全球石油供應暴跌 1,010 萬桶/日
至每日 9,700 萬桶，其中 OPEC+產量月減 940 萬桶/日至每日 4,240 萬桶，非 OPEC+供應月減 77

萬桶/日至每日 5,470 萬桶，儘管沙烏地西岸、阿聯酋富查伊拉港及伊拉克經土耳其傑伊漢的 ITP 管
線替代路線出口量，已從戰前每日不到 400 萬桶提升至每日 720 萬桶，然而仍無法彌補荷莫茲海峽
的供給中斷，使得中東海灣國家石油出口損失合計仍高達每日 1,300 萬桶，自開戰以來，3 月石油供
給損失已超過 3.6 億桶，國際能源署更進一步預估 4 月石油供給損失將擴大至 4.4 億桶。
在供給大幅下降的同時，國際能源署也同步下修全球石油需求預估，預計 2026 年全年需求將年
減 8 萬桶/日，較上月報告下修 73 萬桶/日，其中 3 月全球需求預計將年減 80 萬桶/日，4 月進一步
擴大至年減 230 萬桶/日，合計第二季需求預估年減 150 萬桶/日，為新冠疫情以來最大單季跌幅，
下調需求預估主因荷莫茲海峽供給中斷，導致眾多仰賴中東石油進口的亞洲塑化廠面臨斷供，在缺乏
原料的狀況下被迫降低需求。
然而儘管目前荷莫茲海峽持續受阻，導致油價居高不下，然而戰後有利油價快速下行的因子也持

續在萌芽。阿聯酋 4/28 宣布退出 OPEC 及 OPEC+，為該組織近年來最重要的成員流失。阿聯酋目
前石油產能為 485 萬桶/日 ， 然而其受限於OPEC 配額 ， 實際產量僅約360 萬桶/日 ， 兩者差距達125
萬桶/日，過去幾年來阿聯酋持續因產量受限問題，而與其他成員國出現爭執，隨阿聯酋退出 OPEC，
將有利於其提升產量上限。雖然目前因荷莫茲海峽仍處封鎖狀態，阿聯酋石油出口同樣受阻，然而一
旦戰後海峽重開，阿聯酋將可不再受 OPEC 配額約束，立即釋放約 125 桶/日的增量供給，阿聯酋國
有石油公司 ADNOC 更規劃將在 2027 年將石油產量上升至 500 萬桶/日、2029 年再進一步上升至
600 萬桶/日。此外阿聯酋退出 OPEC 亦可能引發其他成員跟進 ，使得戰後油價下行風險將顯著增加。
展望後市，當前油價雖受到海峽封鎖影響，中短線上有望持續維持在 100 美元左右震盪，然而
美伊戰事未見進一步擴大，後續油價也難以再創新高，此外若未來美伊談判出現進展，海峽運量出現

緩解，阿聯酋增產也有利戰後原油供需恢復，故我們目前對於能源類股後市中性看待。

黃金類股：全球央行續買黃金，惟短線金價仍受美伊戰爭不利影響
黃金價格 4 月持續在區間內震盪，黃金類股表現也相對弱勢，主因受到美伊戰爭影響，油價上漲
帶動通膨預期攀升，使得市場對於聯準會降息預期下滑，帶動配置黃金的機會成本上升，同時避險
情緒導致美元走強，也不利於黃金等以美元計價的商品資產表現。此外，受到戰爭影響，土耳其動
用黃金儲備以支應外匯與流動性需求，短線拋售黃金也導致金價承壓。
世界黃金協會數據顯示，2026 年 3 月各國央行申報合計淨賣出 30 公噸黃金，主要就是受到土
耳其拋售 60 公噸所拖累，受此影響，合計第一季全球央行申報黃金淨購買量僅有 2 頓。然而若看加

計未申報買盤量，則是完全不同的狀況，根據世界黃金協會調查顯示，2026 年第一季全球央行黃金
購買量加計未通報買盤後，合計淨購買 244 噸，為自 2024 年第四季以來的新高，同時顯著高於過
去五年平均購買水準的 228 噸，顯示檯面下的世界央行購金趨勢仍未改變。
另一方面，在高盛 4 月 29 日發布的最新報告中，維持了對於 2026 年底黃金目標價 5,400 美
元/盎司的預測。高盛指出，目前金價主要驅動因素包括全球央行持續推進外匯儲備多元化、目前相
對偏低的投機性部位回歸常態化，以及聯準會預計在年內降息 50 個基點等三大支撐。在央行需求方
面，高盛指出，在其央行會議調查中，約 70%的受訪央行預期全球黃金儲備將繼續上升。在風險評
估方面，高盛認為短線風險偏向下行，主要因荷莫茲海峽封鎖若持續 ，可能觸發債市或股市修正，
進而引發黃金部位遭到清算， 然而中長期風險則偏向上行，因 伊朗事件與更廣泛的地緣政治發展 ，
如格陵蘭及委內瑞拉議題，可能加速資金轉向黃金配置。
另一方面，來自穩定幣發行商 Tether 的黃金買盤也值得關注。截至 2026 年 3 月底，Tether 為

支撐 USDT 儲備而持有的黃金市值上升至 198 億美元，約為 132 公噸，較 2025 年 12 月底的 126
公噸再增加約 6 公噸 ，此外 Tether 另持有 22 公噸黃金以支撐其黃金代幣 XAUT， 兩項產品合計持倉
達約 154 公噸 ， 此規模已足以排進全球前20 名黃金持有者 。JPMorgan 貴金屬研究主管 Greg Shearer
指出，Tether 的購金行為已對金價產生實質性影響，其 2025 年全年購買量僅次於波蘭央行，並超越
所有其他央行，顯示加密貨幣機構儲備已成為黃金新一個重要買盤來源
展望後市，中長線而言央行增持黃金的結構性趨勢未變，美伊衝突加上格陵蘭、委內瑞拉等地緣
政治風險，也都有望加速各國對黃金的分散配置需求 ，使得黃金有望獲得更多來自央行買盤的支撐。
然而短線上，在美伊戰爭尚未底定前，黃金機會成本上升及部分央行拋售壓力仍將壓抑短線走勢，
故預估黃金將在區間內震盪，目前對於黃金類股後市抱持中性看法。

固定收益：華許入主 Fed 機率高，美債波動料加大
FOMC 在 4/29 的會議再度將聯邦基金利率目標區間維持在 3.50-3.75%。然而，理事 Miran 主
張降息一碼，而會後聲明中「傾於寬鬆」(easing bias)的措辭也遭三位地區主席(Cleveland 的
Hammack、Minneapolis 的 Kashkari 與 Dallas 的 Logan )反對 ，會議表決出現 4 票異議 ，創 FOMC
逾 30 年來委員立場最分歧的局面。前述三位地區聯儲主席反對寬鬆的措辭，導致市場對 Fed 後續降
息預期顯著弱化。

此外，川普提名的下任 Fed 主席 Kevin Warsh(華許)於 4/29 參議院銀行委員會審查通過，預計
於 5/11 當週進行全院表決。目前參議院共和黨、民主黨的議員比例為 53：47(含兩名傾向民主黨的
無黨籍議員)，由於除了 Tillis 以外的共和黨參議員均未公開就華許的資格表達反對，未來在參院全院
的表決中，華許也可望取得過半票數通過該任命案。若時程安排得宜，料可於 5/15 鮑爾主席任滿後
接掌 Fed。值得留意的是，鮑爾在 4 月的記者會表示將繼續留任理事會，這將導致未來的 FOMC 將
出現新舊主席並存的罕見現象。
綜觀華許的主要貨幣政策主張為「降息」 +「縮表」 ，兩者在貨幣政策方向呈現相反的效果，且
對不同天期的債券影響亦有差異： 「降息」對於雖使債券殖利率下修，但對短端影響較大；而「縮表」
量化緊縮(QT)雖屬貨幣緊縮政策，但堪薩斯聯儲研究(Smith & Valcarcel 2021)顯示，「縮表」推升期
限溢酬的幅度大於短期利率。兩者同時施行可能造成導致未來美債殖利率曲線淨陡峭化。話雖如此，

美國貨幣政策需要 FOMC 票委以多數決通過，前主席 Yellen 認為華許即便就任 Fed 主席，在表決中
仍難以取得足以支持其貨幣政策路線的票數。因此，即便 Warsh 上任，其政策主張(尤其在縮表的議
題上)短期將無法付緒實現，故預期在可見的未來對美國的貨幣政策走向改變有限。
然而，Fed 主席的權力在於議程設定與對外溝通，華許在聽證會上所表達的部分想法-包括降低
FOMC 會議與記者會舉辦的頻率等，將使 FOMC 決策透明度降低，或導致美國債市波動加大。其表
達改變目前聯準會青睞的通膨指標-核心 PCE 的意圖，也可能使金融市場的不確定性增加。
預期短線債市在通膨疑慮與政策不確定性的交錯下 ， 將持續面臨殖利率高檔震盪的壓力， 但考量
美伊全面開戰可能性極低，油價的通膨疑慮料隨時間鈍化，而 Warsh 立場傾向支持降息，因此就中
期來看，美債殖利率應有走降空間。同時，我們認為中短天期債相較於長天期債券殖利率相去不遠，
投資人無需承擔較高的存續期間更可享有不錯的債息收益 ， 且中短天期到期期間較短亦有資金靈活運

用的優勢，因此建議可以中短天期美國公債/投資級債為主。

1 
2408 南亞科(Nanya Technology)

報告日期： 2026/10/13 研 究 員：翁浩軒  (Davy Wong)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 3Q26 毛利率優於預期且將維持 8 成以上 
 2027 年 DRAM 供需仍緊俏 
 新廠 2027 年第四季加入貢獻

一. 3Q26 毛利率優於預期，EPS 25.4 元 
南亞科 3Q26 營收 1336.49 億(QoQ+61.9% 、YoY+611.7%) ，其中位元
出貨量季減 4~6% ， 平均銷售單價季增超過 60%(主要因反應長期合
約重新簽訂之調整)，匯率影響有利 1~3%，3Q26 毛利率上升至
85.7%，主要因平均銷售單價上升，營業費用季增 28 億元，因當
期及未來的員工薪酬增加，稅後淨利 876.27 億(QoQ+74.58%、
YoY+5505.16%)，EPS 25.4 元。

二. 4Q26 整體產業仍將維持供不應求態勢  
受惠於 AI 需求 ， AI 運算（GPU 、 TPU 、 CPU 及 ASIC）成為 DRAM 
需求成長主力，帶動高容量、高頻寬及客製化記憶體架構需求，
範疇從 HBM 、LPDDR5 進一步擴展至網路、儲存與系統管理等領
域。在傳統應用市場方面，由於 DRAM 供給不足與 ASP 上漲，
部分傳統應用終端出現產品降規或延長生命週期的現象，低階市
場則有回收 DRAM 支撐部分需求。供給端方面，因新廠建置、設
備交期、製程導入均需要較長的前置時間，目前供給仍維持緊俏，
智慧型手機、個人電腦及一般消費性記憶體供給受限，製造商與
供應鏈端的庫存水位普遍偏低 ， 隨著供應商依 LTA 長約進行產品
組合與產能規劃，有利未來供需穩定度。

三. 新廠預估 4Q27 開始投產 
公司 5A 新廠預計 2027 年第一季裝機，2027 年第四季開始投
產 ， 預計 2028 年底前達到每月投片量 3 萬片 ， 全產能目標於 2030 
年底前達每月 5 萬片，未來產能擴充均以客戶長期合約的 AI 專
案為主要目標。另外公司亦佈局先進封測領域，規劃於屏東科學
園區建置 3D 晶圓封裝與測試廠 ， 預估 2H28 設備進駐 。 公司 2026
年前三季累計資本支出為 276 億元 ， 2026 年資本支出以不超過 697
億元為上限，其中生產設備資本支出約占五成。

四.  2026 / 2027 年獲利創高，預估 EPS 77.1 元/ 134.3 元 
投資評等：買進 
目標價位：670 元 
       5 X 2027 EPS 
參考價位：527.0 元 
     3.92 X 2027 EPS

展望 2026 下半年至 2027 年，AI 需求維持強勁，AI 及一般雲端伺
服器 DRAM 需求持續擴大 ， 接下來逐步擴散至非雲端市場 ， 小型
語言模型推廣 AI 至邊緣裝置，新增客製化 DRAM 需求，整體記
憶體需求持續上揚。供給方面，2026 年產業微幅增加產能，預估
2027 年新增產能有限，多種 DRAM 產品將持續缺貨，三大原廠新
增產能實際貢獻要到 2028 年才會顯現 。 南亞科新廠預計 2028 年放
量，初期每月投片量 3 萬片，全產能將達 5 萬片/月。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 670 元 
南亞科為全球前五大 DRAM 廠，受惠於 AI 帶動之供給端改善，記
憶體產品漲價快速，南亞科今明年獲利創高，2028 年全球雖然有
新產能開出，但隨著 AI 由模型訓練與推理延伸至更多客製化邊緣
AI 應用，帶動記憶體位元需求長期成長。考量到 AI 應用滲透率提
升帶動記憶體需求，記憶體已成戰略物資，客戶亦開始簽訂長約，
參考 2027 年 EPS 給予 5 倍本益比，目標價 670 元，建議買進

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
南亞科成立於 1 995 年，為 DRAM （動態隨機存取記憶體）之研
發、設計、製造與銷售商，為全球第四大 DRAM 廠，市佔率約 
3%，由美光授權製程技術，其十二吋晶圓廠位於泰山南林園區，
月產能 6.8 萬片 ， 其中 20nm 製程約 3.8 萬片 ， 30nm 製程約 3 萬
片，公司產品廣泛應用於手機、PC、消費性電子產品，主要競爭
對手包括 SK Hynix、三星電子、美光、華邦電等。

二.產業趨勢概況  
1. AI 帶動記憶體價格雙位數上漲 
全球 CSP 大廠持續加強投資 AI server 及相關基礎建設，全球九大
CSP 廠之資本支出持續高速成長，2026 年資本支出將達 9220 億元
(YoY+98%)，2027 年資本支出成長至 13830 億美元(YoY+50%)。
TrendForce 預估 2026 年 HBM 需求量年增 70%。而隨著 AI 應用由
LLM 模型訓練延伸至推論，CSP 廠擴大通用型伺服器布建，也使
記憶體拉貨重心由 HBM 延伸至 RDIMM ， 進一步推升 DDR5 價格。
3Q26 在 AI 與資料中心建置需求持續下記憶體仍嚴重供不應求 ， 儘
管價格基期已高，但預估 DRAM 合約價持續上漲，TrendForce 預
估第三季整體 conventional DRAM 合約價上漲 13-18% ， 第四季持續
上漲 10%~15%。供給方面，DRAM 三大原廠今年新增產能有限，
三星及海力士均以既有廠區調整及製程轉換為主，預估晶圓產能
分別增加 5%及 8%，而美光產能持平，三大原廠新廠產能開出並

明顯貢獻預估在 2028 年 。 預估 2026 年 DRAM 價格逐季調升 ， 2027
年新增供給有限下，價格易漲難跌。

2. 2027 年記憶體需求維持強勁 
展望 2026 第四季至 2027 年，AI 需求維持強勁，AI 及一般雲端伺
服器 DRAM 需求持續擴大，接下來逐步擴散至非雲端市場 (如: 
Edge、客製化)，小型語言模型推廣 AI 至邊緣裝置 (PC、手機、汽
車、機器人等)，新增客製化 DRAM 需求，整體記憶體需求持續
上揚。需求面依各應用別而言，Server 方面，AI 及高效能運算持
續驅動 HBM 與 DDR5、LPDDR5 需求，高階 e-SSD、BMC、NIC
對 DDR4、LPDDR4 及 DDR5 需求顯著; PC 及手機方面，高階 PC
與手機對 DDR5 及 LPDDR5 需求維持穩定 ， 然中低階需求疲弱; 消
費型電子市場方面，AI 及一般伺服器對 HBM 、RDIMM 需求持續
擴張，排擠一般消費型記憶體供給。供給方面，2026 年產業微幅
增加產能，聚焦於高階雲端與高附加價值產品需求，DRAM 大廠
優先分配產能於 DDR5 及 HBM 產品 ， 預估 2027 年新增產能有限，
多種 DRAM 產品將持續缺貨，三大原廠新增產能實際貢獻要到
2028 年才會顯現 。 南亞科第二代 10 奈米級製程(1B) 16Gb DDR5 產
品交運量已達 10%，第三代 10 奈米級(1C)及第四代 10 奈米級(1D)
製程技術試產中。公司 DDR5 營收約占 10%，並可視需求彈性調
整增加，同時持續供應 DDR4 及 LPDDR4 產品以緩解市場缺口。
公司亦積極與策略夥伴合作拓展 AI 邊緣運算驅動的記憶體市場，
目前處工程驗證階段，預計於 2026 年底推出新產品。南亞科 2025
年資本支出 134 億元，2026 年資本支出預計不超過 697 億元，其
中生產設備支出約占五成，新廠預計於 2027 年第一季開始裝機，
2027 年第四季開始生產，2028 年放量，初期每月投片量 3 萬片，
預估 2030 年全產能將達 5 萬片/月。南亞科 2025 年位元銷售量成
長 50 多%，預估 2026 年成長 17~19%，預估 2027 年成長中低個位
數。

YoY+5505.16%)，EPS 25.4 元。

2、2026/2027 年營收獲利創高，預估 EPS 77.1 / 134.3 元

Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
Takeaways on NPO from 2026
OCP Global Summit
Morgan Stanley Asia Limited+
Andy Meng, CFA
Equity Analyst
Andy.Meng@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7689
Betty Chen
Research Associate
Betty.H.Chen@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7213
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
On Day 2 of the 2026 OCP Global Summit, an IPEC optical interconnect workshop

focused on discussions around NPO. Industry participants such as Source Photonics,
Lightcounting, Alibaba, Meta, Oracle, Microsoft, Ciena, Ligent, Genuine Optics,
Lumentum, Lumilens, NVIDIA and Huawei presented their views on NPO.
The industry has formed the view connectivity, rather than compute alone, is
becoming the primary bottleneck. Near-packaged optics (NPO) move optical
conversion closer to compute, targeting better signal integrity, bandwidth, and

energy efficiency. OpenCPX explicitly targets common mechanical, electrical,
optical, thermal, and management interfaces across suppliers, providing the
ecosystem with a shared implementation framework rather than only a broad
architectural vision.
However, we also observed debate over whether pluggable NPO offers materially
better flexibility and serviceability than CPO. Key concern on CPO is that optical
failure could result in scrapping an entire ASIC assembly.

Overall, we observed growing discussion and interests in NPO, implying a greater
likelihood of accelerated commercialization over the next 12-18 months, in our view.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Connectivity has become the bottleneck, and NPO is a potential solution. We
observed growing discussion and interest in NPO at the conference.
October 14, 2026 08:47 AM GMT

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

2026/10/19 投資速報

營收走勢圖

研究員/張均壕 JUN_HAO1@uni-psg.com

研究部仍看好時碩未來營運，主要是考量液冷散熱零件業務隨著客
戶訂單逐步放量及出貨規模擴大 ，帶動 2H26 業績大幅提升。 此外 ，
航太事業成長前景仍佳，工業事業亦可望持續增長，明年展望依舊
正向。研究部預估時碩 2026 年 EPS 3.52 元(+162%YoY)，2027 年
EPS 4.63 元(+32%YoY)。以明年獲利為評價基礎，給予 20 倍本益
比，目標價 93 元。

重點摘要：

1. 受惠AI趨勢，液冷散熱訂單將帶動3Q26業績強勁增長： 
時碩3Q26合併營收上升至18.18億元(+24%QoQ，+43%YoY)，主
要是受惠AI資料中心液冷相關零組件的貢獻增加，令7、8、9
月單月營收持續創新高 。考量營收擴大， 規模效應顯現， 預期
時碩3Q26毛利率將提升至 24.5%，營益率提升至 8.8%，預估
3Q26稅後淨利1.38億元(+101%QoQ，+34%YoY)，預估3Q26EPS 
1.26元。

2. 液冷散熱產品進入爬坡期，4Q26營運淡季不淡： 
展望4Q26， 雖然年底通常為時碩汽車、工業業務的淡季， 但受
惠散熱零件仍處在出貨爬坡期，預期季度營收與獲利仍有望
再墊高。研究部預估時碩 4Q26合併營收18.85億元(+4%QoQ，
+55%YoY)，4Q26稅後淨利1.40億元(+1%QoQ，+113%YoY)， 預估
4Q26EPS 1.27元 。 全年度來看 ， 研究部預估時碩2026年合併營
收將提升至65.25億元(+31%YoY)，年度毛利率24.1%，營益率
7.1%，稅後淨利3.87億元(+161%YoY)，預估2026年EPS 3.52
元。

3. 航太業務維持成長動能，積極拓展新商機 
受惠於全球航太產業需求穩健及上游關鍵零組件產能吃緊，
時碩管理層持續看好航太業務前景 ，其中天陽航太營運成長
率預估可達15%～20%。 此外 ， 公司積極擴充泰國航太產能 ， 規
劃建置鋁合金及不鏽鋼生產線，以掌握全球航太供應鏈重組
及非紅供應鏈商機 ， 中長期目標為貢獻約10億元營收 。 除既有
航太業務外，時碩亦積極拓展人形機器人及半導體設備等新
興應用領域。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

在人形機器人方面， 時碩已投入諧波減速器及關節零組件開
發，並與國際及中國大陸主要廠商展開合作。半導體設備方
面，時碩持續開發相關精密零組件，未來將配合泰國新廠布
局 ， 爭取台灣客戶海外工廠的供應商機 。 整體而言 ， 隨著航太
業務穩健成長，以及人形機器人與半導體設備等新領域逐步
拓展 ， 將有助於公司建立更多元的營運成長動能 ， 降低對傳統
汽車產業的依賴。

4. 明年業績續升，預估2027年EPS 4.63元： 
展望2027年 ， 考量日系大廠的液冷散熱訂單將延續至明年 ， 而
且還有美系散熱大廠客戶訂單與磁浮壓縮機關鍵訂單等待發
酵 ， 加上航太業務仍具偏強增速 ， 預期時碩2027年業績將持續
提升。研究部預估時碩2027年合併營收74.20億元(+14%YoY)，
年度毛利率 25.0%， 預估2027年EPS將進一步提升至 4.63元。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

告 中 所 有 的 意 見 及 預 估 ， 如 有 變 更 恕 不 另 行 通 知 。

供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資
料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 作 出 任 何
陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非

### Page 1

本刊載之報告為元富投顧於特定日期之分析，已力求陳述內容之可靠性，純屬研究性質，僅作參考，使用者應明瞭內容之時效性，
審慎考量投資風險，並就投資結果自行負責。報告著作權屬元富投顧所有，禁止任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。 
元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號 
公司拜訪快報

百和(9938) 
建議：維持買進 
結論：最壞已過且股價低於淨值，靜待基本面起飛

一、3Q26 自結 EPS 0.62 元，低於預期；主要受到網
布毛利率下滑且業外利息費用所致 
百和 3Q26 營收 44.09 億元(+15.9% QoQ / +5.8% YOY)，
就各產品比重來看，黏扣帶 12.3%、織鞋帶 44.1%、鬆
緊帶 13%、射出勾 6.6%、針織經編提花網布 20.1%以
及其他 3.9%。3Q26 就各產品線來看，所有產品線均成
長，僅有經編網布衰退，其中鬆緊帶+16%YoY、黏扣帶
+9.7%YoY、織鞋帶+9% YOY、射出勾+6.7%YoY 以及
針織經編提花網布-8.4% YOY。獲利方面，3Q26 毛利
率 29.5%，與 2Q26 約略持平，也較3Q25 下滑 6.3%，
主要反映關稅影響、產品組合以及受到美伊戰爭影響原
物料成本上揚所致；就 3Q26 各產品線毛利率來看，粘
扣帶 27.6%、 鬆緊帶37.9%、 射出勾44%、 織鞋帶30.4%、
經編網布 17.2％以及其他 37.5%。其中經編網布毛利率
較 2Q26 回升 2.7%，但仍低於 2025 年水準，主要反映
產品組合差異以及稼動率的影響；3Q26 合併營業利益
達 4.35 億元，營業利益率 9.9%，業外淨損 1.41 億元，
主要反映百和-KY 利息費用，稅前淨利 2.94 億元，稅後
歸屬母公司淨利 1.84 億元，EPS 0.62 元。  
二 、 考量原物料成本提升且通膨持續影響全球消費景氣 ，
下修百和 2026 年營收為低個位數增長且因毛利率表現
低於預期，下修 2026 年 EPS 為 2.48 元 
9M26 前十大客戶佔比 65.5%，德國品牌仍為第一大客
戶 ， 佔比25.1%，YOY-5%、 美系品牌15.7%，YOY+2%，
其中鞋子持平、服飾類+11%、瑞士品牌為第三大客戶，
佔營收比重 6.1%，YOY-11%，New Balance 占比 4.4%，
YOY-11%。HOKA 已為前十大客戶 ， 佔營收比重2.3%，
YOY+58%，為前九月成長性最佳客戶。就產品應用別
來看 ，前九月Footwear 占比 60.4%、Apparel 25.3%、
3C 2.8%； 以前九個月YOY 來看 ，Footwear-1% YOY、
Apparel +12% YOY、3C 產品+16% YOY 以及醫療-3% 
YOY，但單就第三季表現來看，Footwear 持平、
Apparel+27% YOY、3C+20% YOY 以及醫療+31% 
YOY，顯示其他應用端需求起來，未來百和將持續著重
其他類別發展，不單僅限於鞋子跟衣服，醫療跟 3C 已
漸有成效。過去 3C 是客人指定材料，百和僅有加工；
但現在幾乎 80%都是百和的材料進行加工，做成客戶想
要的東西，附加價值較高。3C 及醫療目前營收占比約
6%，未來可望緩步增加。 
考量美伊戰爭引發原物料成本提升且戰爭仍未平息影響
全球消費景氣，輔以客戶下單仍顯保守；我們預估百和
2026 年營收為 161.25 億元 (+2.2% YoY)，其中傳產中
個位數成長、網布中~高個位數衰退；毛利率方面，考量
原物料價格上揚因素未解、關稅影響持續且經編網布毛
利率低於預期 ， 保守預估2026 年集團毛利率為 30.7%，
較 2025 年下滑 4.6%，下修本業獲利為 17.68 億元，營
業利益率 11%，業外利息費用與 2025 年大致相當，預
估稅後歸屬母公司淨利潤 7.41 億元 ， 下修EPS 為 2.48
元。 
資本支出方面，2025 年實際資本支出 19.01 億元，其中
廠房建物 26％、設備購置 72%以及其他 2%。9M26 資
本支出 22.69 億元，其中台灣百和3.31 億元、百和興業
19.38 億元；2026 年預期資本支出約 28.02 億元，主要
以百和興業為主，約占 3/4；台灣百和約 1/4，主要用在
機器設備。 
三、下修經編緹花網布 2026 年至中~高個位數衰退，長
期仍可望有轉單效益 
2025 年隨著品牌採用意願提升，網布營收貢獻達 34.08
億元，YOY+38%，毛利率 34.09%；2026 年原預估在
新產能以及核心客戶、新興客戶放量且打入成衣供應鏈
帶動，成長性不亞於 2025 年，但受到全球大環境不確
定性高且受到產品組合以及主要客戶新一代跑鞋主料轉
用梭織且非由百和-KY 供應影響，前九個月網布營收衰
4.9%，我們下修 2026 年經編緹花網布營收為 31.46 億
元，-7.68% YOY， 毛利率受到稼動率偏低且產品組合影
響，前三季僅 19.9%。現在網布往圓編、橫編等更多織
法開發，未來可望持續擴大應用面，未來隨著應用面擴
大且稼動率提升，毛利率有機會往 2025 年水準邁進。 
網布客戶占比來看 ，2025 年德系品牌仍是第一大客戶，

元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號

四、維持買進理由 
2026 年受到美伊戰爭引發原物料成本提升且戰爭仍未
平息影響全球消費景氣，輔以客戶下單仍顯保守；我們
預估百和 2026 年營收為 161.25 億元 (+2.2% YoY)，其
中傳產中個位數成長 、 網布中~高個位數衰退 ， 下修EPS
為 2.48 元。展望 2027 年，隨著品牌對應庫存調整到較
健康的水位，下單可望回到正常模式。大環境穩定後，
原物料成本可以趨於正常、關稅議題減緩，2027 年可望
優於 2026 年，預估 2027 年 EPS 3.23 元。股價方面，
考量最壞已過 、2027 年可望恢復成長且目前股價低於淨
值，故維持買進評等，目標價 48 元(15X 2027PER)。

DS부문 예상 영업이익은 100.0조원(OPM 67.4%, YoY +1538.5%, QoQ +14.5%) 으로, 
달러환율 1,380원기준, 메모리와 비메모리 사업부문 예상 영업손익은 각각 102.0조원,    
-2.0조원으로 추정한다. 1) 메모리반도체 가격 상승세가 이어지는 가운데, HBM4 판매 
본격화에 따른 영향을 고려하면 DRAM/NAND 분기 평균 가격 상승폭은 각각 +18.0%, 
+16.0%으로 추정하고, 2)비메모리반도체 사업 적자는 지속될 전망이기 때문이다. 다만 
특별 성과급 지급에 따른 충당금 영향을 제외하고 선단공정 중심의 파운드리 가동률을 
고려하면 적자폭은 유의미하게 감소한 것으로 파악된다는 점이 긍정적이다.   
SDC부문 예상 영업이익은 1.1조원(OPM 13.0%, YoY -9.6%, QoQ +54.9%)으로, 1)국내 
주력 고객사향 폴더블 스마트폰 판매 호조가 사업부의 외형 성장에 긍정적으로 작용했을 
것으로 추정됨에도 불구하고, 2) 8.6세대 IT OLED 패널 양산 개시에 따른 감가상각비(약 
0.3조원 수준)와 북미 고객 중심의 판가 인하 압박 영향으로 전년동기대비 수익성은 약 
2%pt 훼손이 불가피할 전망이다. DX부문 예상 영업손익은 -1.1조원(YoY 적자전환, QoQ 
적자지속)으로, 메모리반도체를 포함한 IT 부품 가격 부담이 전분기대비 확대되고 있기 
때문이다. MX와 VD/DA사업부 예상 손익은 각각 -0.9조원, -0.2조원으로 추정한다.

투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 63만원으로 상향 조정  
메모리반도체의 가격 상승폭이 다소 둔화되겠지만, 2027~2028년에도 타이트한 수급 
환경이 이어질 전망이라는 점을 주목한다. 1)시장은 신규 증설과 HBM 관련 노이즈로 인한 
2028년 피크아웃 가능성을 우려하고 있으나, HBM 의 범용 DRAM 생산능력 잠식과 AI 
추론 확산에 따른 고용량 메모리 수요를 감안하면 공급 제약의 해소 시점은 예상보다 
늦어질 가능성이 높기 때문이다. 뿐만 아니라, 2)메모리 업사이클이 장기화될 경우 동사 
FCF는 과거대비 구조적으로 확대되어 보다 친화적인 주주환원 정책(2027~2029년, 
2027년 1월 예정)을 제시할 가능성도 배제할 수 없기 때문이다. 동사 2027년 연간 실적 
추정치 상향 조정에 따라 목표주가를 63만원(2027년 BPS * Target PBR 3.5배 적용)으로 
상향 조정한다. 피크아웃 우려에 따른 주가 조정은 중장기 관점에서 매수 기회다.

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 
2026-10-14

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 820,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 366,500 원 
상승여력: 123.7%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 및 환율 영향으로 시장 예상치 하회 예상 
Key: 4Q26부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 계약 수주 모멘텀 기대 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 4 행정 엔진&FDC으로 주가 상승여력 
충분. 매수

3Q26 Preview: 컨센서스 하회하는 실적 기록 예상 
3Q26 연결 매출액 6조 464억원(+36.9% YoY, -4.5% QoQ), 영업이익 9,230억
원(+65.6% YoY, -11.2% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(1조 70억원)를 하회하는 실적 기
록할 것으로 추정한다. 이전 분기 대비 중대재해 및 하계휴가, 그리고 추석 연휴에 따른 
조업일수 감소 효과와 더불어 큰 폭의 환율 하락 영향(약 500억원) 때문이다. 홍범도
함 화재 및 임단협 관련 일회성 비용은 이번 분기가 아닌 4Q 에 반영될 것으로 예상한
다. 해양 부문에서 트리온 FPU 프로젝트 C/O에 따른 일부 환입 발생한 것으로 추정
되며, 해양 도크에서 컨테이너선 선체 블록 일부 건조로 고정비 감소효과 일부 상쇄 중
이다. 다만 올해 연내 혹은 내년 초 추가 해양 수주 없을 시 고정비 상승 우려 확대될 
전망이다. 동사 올해 상선 수주 목표(114.7억불)를 이미 초과하는 수주 실적 기록한 상
황이며, 고마진 선종인 LNGC 및 VLGC/VLAC 중심으로 수주했기 때문에 ‘28~’29
년 구간 경쟁사 대비 뚜렷한 선종 믹스 효과를 기대한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 82 만원으로 하향 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 97 만원에서 82
만원으로 하향한다. 중국의 공격적인 캐파 증설 지속과 더불어 중국 대형 조선사들의 
글로벌 선주가 발주한 LNGC 물량 및 원가 경쟁력 확대에 따른 상선 부문의 멀티플 
조정이 불가피하다는 판단이다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클
과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 FDC에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지
속되고 있는 중으로 파악된다. 올해 4 분기부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 부문의 본격적
인 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 전망이며, 해당 모멘텀에 따른 주가 상승
여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)를 유지한다.

작성자(한승한)는 본 조사분석자료에 게재된 내용들이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 신의성실하게 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다.

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI交易崩盘，Anthropic会推迟_超级IPO_吗？距离11月中旬_预定上市日期_不远了.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-24
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

时间窗⼝极度紧张 —— 按 SEC 规定，最晚下周须提交 S-1 ⽂件，预测市场给出年内上市概率
约 80% 。 AI 板块遭抛售令上市前景蒙阴，此前 9 ⽉上市计划已告流产。多位市场⼈⼠表⽰，
Anthropic 可能⾯临推迟上市的压⼒，但也有分析师指出，该公司对资本的迫切需求或将推
动其坚持原定计划。
摘要：
AI 独⻆兽 Anthropic 正站在上市的历史关⼝，但市场逆⻛正在加剧不确定性。
据路透社报道， Claude 聊天机器⼈的开发商 Anthropic 计划于11 ⽉中旬完成 IPO ，⽬标估值⾼达2
万亿美元，有望超越 SpaceX 今年早些时候 1.77 万亿美元的上市规模，成为史上最⼤科技 IPO 之
⼀。然⽽，美国证券交易委员会（ SEC ）要求公司在启动路演前⾄少 15 天公开提交招股说明
书，这意味着 Anthropic留给⾃⼰的窗⼝期正在迅速收窄。
与此同时，AI 板块近期遭遇明显抛售——CoreWeave 股价两周内暴跌约 30% ，美光过去⼀个⽉
下跌 17%—— 市场对 AI ⽀出可持续性的疑虑持续升温。多位市场⼈⼠表⽰， Anthropic 可能⾯临

推迟上市的压⼒，但也有分析师指出，该公司对资本的迫切需求或将推动其坚持原定计划。预
测市场⽬前显⽰， Anthropic 年内上市的概率约为80%。
时间窗⼝：最晚下周提交 S-1 ⽂件
根据 SEC 规定，公司须在路演启动前⾄少 15 天公开提交招股说明书（ S-1 ⽂件）。佛罗⾥达⼤学
IPO 研究项⽬负责⼈ Jay Ritterh 表⽰，若 Anthropic 希望赶在感恩节前完成上市，时间仍然充裕，
但已相当紧张。
具体⽽⾔，若公司将 IPO 定于最晚 11 ⽉ 24 ⽇，并于 11 ⽉ 16 ⽇启动路演，则须在下周末前公开提
交 S-1 ⽂件。
值得注意的是， Anthropic 此前曾被预期在劳⼯节（ Labor Day ）后不久公开披露招股说明书，彼
时市场预计其可能于 9 ⽉下旬挂牌上市，但该计划并未如期推进。
市场逆⻛： AI 板块承压，上市窗⼝趋窄
据 MarketWatch 报道，AI 相关股票近期的⼤幅回调，是 Anthropic 管理层不得不正视的重要变
量。
新兴云计算公司 CoreWeave 因市场担忧其债务管理能⼒，股价在过去两周内下跌约 30% ；存储

芯⽚巨头美光过去⼀个⽉跌幅达 17% ，折射出市场对 AI ⽀出规模及融资前景的⼴泛忧虑。
Rational Equity Armor Fund 投资组合经理 Joe Tigay 表⽰，持续的通胀压⼒与⾼企的能源价格同
样令⼈担忧。
然⽽，也有声⾳认为， Anthropic 的上市对于提振市场信⼼⾄关重要。
“ 从历史经验来看，增⻓放缓与通胀并存的组合往往是股市出现裂缝的前兆。如果这些压⼒持续
积累， Anthropic 完全有理由选择继续保持私有状态，等待更好的时机。 ”

Cerity Partners ⾸席市场策略师 Jim Lebenthal 指出， AI ⾏业的⻓期⽣命⼒与商业模式近期受到
更多质疑，市场需要⼀次成功的 Anthropic IPO 来证明 AI 具备持续发展的能⼒。
“ 提出疑问是合理的，甚⾄产⽣怀疑也是合理的， ”Lebenthal 表⽰，“ 但整体股票市场的健康以及
美国经济的健康，都在很⼤程度上依赖于⼈⼯智能⾏业的成功。 ”
资本需求：上市是获取资⾦的最现实路径
抛开市场情绪， Anthropic ⾃⾝对资本的迫切需求，或许才是推动其坚持上市计划的核⼼动⼒。
Zacks Investment Research 股票策略师 Andrew Rocco 表⽰，最⼤的 AI 公司已经表明，“ 它们基

本上愿意尽其所能地⼤规模投⼊，以争取更多⽤⼾，并试图成为主导性的 AI 玩家 ”。
他认为， Anthropic 将努⼒推进上市， “ 因为它们确实需要那笔资⾦，尤其是在私募市场融资可能
变得更加困难的情况下 ” 。
华尔街⻅闻⽂章写道，据《华尔街⽇报》援引 Anthropic 招股说明书草案显⽰，该公司未来数年
在 AI 算⼒⽅⾯的⽀出规模可能⾼达7500 亿美元。与此同时，招股书显⽰， Anthropic 在 2026 年上
半年录得超过800 亿美元的净亏损，营收为 163 亿美元，不过该亏损主要由会计调整驱动，⽽⾮
基本业务层⾯的恶化。
预测市场数据显⽰， Anthropic 在年内完成上市的概率约为80%。

Document 273: ECB caught in the French drama

Source PDF: all report pdf/ECB caught in the French drama.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

The European Central Bank meeting on 29 October is not
expected to result in an interest rate hike. Instead, the French
crisis is increasingly eroding the need for additional rate hikes
Carsten Brzeski
With the surge in bond yields on the back of the crisis in France, we expect the
ECB to remain on hold at next week's meeting
Back in September, the ECB surprised markets with a more hawkish
message, at least between the lines. With headline inﬂation higher

for longer and core inﬂation expected to increase in 2027, the stage
seemed set for at least one more rate hike at the December
meeting. Some even speculated about a back-to-back increase in
October.
One month later, and after a severe ﬁscal and political crisis in
France, the ECB is unlikely to repeat the hawkish tone at next week’s
meeting. At face value, macro developments since the last meeting
would still justify another rate hike. Headline inﬂation increased

again in September and most leading indicators point to continued
resilience in the eurozone economy. However, the surge in bond

### Page 2

yields on the back of the French crisis is already imposing a new
restrictive monetary policy stance. In fact, the surge in German
bond yields since the September meeting has already had roughly
the same impact on growth and inﬂation as an additional 25bp
rate hike. For France, it's closer to a 50bp hike. Or in other words, the
bond market has taken over the ECB’s job.
All of this means that the ECB is very likely to stay on hold next
week and will delay any next rate hike decision to December. This is

when new staﬀ projections will be available and when there will
also be more insights into whether higher energy prices will
eventually bring knock-on eﬀects on the rest of the economy or
not. In fact, there are three main parameters that, in our view, will
determine the ECB’s path going forward: i) the length and intensity
of the war in the Middle East. The longer oil prices remain elevated,
the higher the probability of more rate hikes; ii) the knock-on eﬀects

of higher energy prices. So far, there has hardly been any pass-
through of higher energy prices in the rest of the economy. If this
remains the case, the need for further rate hikes drops. If it doesn’t,
the likelihood of new rate hikes goes up. iii) France. Any solution to
the current French ﬁscal woes could bring some relief to bond
markets and allow the ECB to continue hiking rates. Without any
solution, bond markets have taken over the ECB’s role, making rate

hikes less likely (and less needed).
Because it's France
Even if France does not play a role in the ECB’s policy statement
next week, it will be an important element at the press conference.
Political and ﬁscal concerns in France, and the resulting widening in
French spreads, have raised questions about whether the ECB could
intervene through the Transmission Protection Instrument (TPI),
which allows it to purchase securities issued by member states

whose ﬁnancing conditions have deteriorated for reasons not
justiﬁed by country-speciﬁc fundamentals. However, before
activating it, the Governing Council considers a cumulative set of
criteria to determine whether the countries concerned are pursuing
sound and sustainable ﬁscal and macroeconomic policies. In
practice, this includes compliance with the EU ﬁscal framework,

which would, at a minimum, require the proposed French budget to
be fully adopted.
The ECB has nevertheless left itself some discretion, stating that the
intervention criteria “will be dynamically adjusted to the unfolding
risks and conditions to be addressed”. This brings back memories of
the famous “because it’s France” by former European Commission
President Jean-Claude Juncker. Generally speaking, the ECB will be
in a diﬃcult position: intervening too quickly in support of France

may be viewed unfavourably by other member states and
interpreted as monetary ﬁnancing of the debt of a country
unwilling to follow the rules. That argues strongly against early
intervention. Also, don’t forget that the leadership changes at the
ECB over the next months (Schnabel, Lane, Lagarde) could also
aﬀect how the ECB views TPI.
Given the challenges of justifying the activation of TPI at present, we
expect the ECB to adopt a three-pronged approach to address the

French crisis.
1. The ﬁrst line of defence is a more dovish approach to policy
rates. As bond markets are currently taking over the ECB’s job
of inserting a more restrictive monetary stance on the
economy, there is less need for the ECB to hike rates
aggressively, or to hike rates at all. In fact, the ECB has already
started to prepare for this option, as ECB president Christine
Lagarde recently noted that “while growth has been resilient,
since our last meeting long-term interest rates have risen

notably, which will slow growth and reduce pass-through by
more than projected in our September exercise".
2. The second line of defence would be a covert return to bond
purchases. The ECB could instead pause quantitative tightening
temporarily and reinvest maturing bonds in its portfolio
'ﬂexibly', sending a positive signal to bond markets. This had
already happened during the Covid period.
3. Last but not least, should contagion in eurozone bond markets

intensify, we expect the ECB to eventually trigger the TPI. As
long as France remains non-compliant with the European ﬁscal

rules, the ECB might even consider purchasing bonds from
almost all eurozone countries, except for France.

All in all, next week’s ECB meeting looks set to be uneventful on
action but clearly not on words.

Document 279: El Niño 2026–2027_ multiple pressures on prices and supplies (2026-09-30)

Source PDF: all report pdf/El Niño 2026–2027_ multiple pressures on prices and supplies (2026-09-30).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

30 September 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
EL NIÑO 2026–2027: MULTIPLE PRESSURES ON PRICES AND SUPPLIES
Hélène Drouot
The 2026–2027 El Niño climate event is expected to be one of the most intense in the last fifty years, with sea-surface-temperature
anomalies potentially exceeding +3°C in the equatorial Pacific. According to the World Meteorological Organisation (WMO), El Niño
is expected to persist until February 2027, with asymmetrical effects, as South-East Asia and northern Latin America will experience

droughts, while southern Brazil and Argentina could see heavy rainfall. Above all, El Niño is compounding the effects of global warming
and exacerbating existing extreme-weather events (droughts, floods and heatwaves). The economic effects of this climate shock mainly
play out through three avenues: agricultural commodity prices, energy and logistics (see heatmap).
Agricultural commodity prices: volatility is causing tensions, with inflationary effects

In India and South-East Asia, droughts will affect harvests, pushing up prices (particularly for coffee, cocoa, rice and palm oil). Conversely, in
southern Brazil and Argentina, heavy rainfall will boost soya and maize production, putting downward pressure on prices. According to the IMF,
El Niño causes global food prices to rise by between 5% (in a typical episode) and 8% (very high intensity) year-on-year, with the full impact being
felt 6 to 12 months after the climatic peak.

Therefore, for a given country, the weighting of food in its inflation index (CPI) plays a decisive role. As a result, all other things being equal, an 8%
rise in prices adds around 1 pp to inflation when food accounts for 13% of the CPI basket (average for countries shown in green on the heatmap),
1.8 pp when this share reaches 22% (yellow), 2.8 pp at 35% (orange) and nearly 4 pp above 49% (red).
The risks are skewed to the upside, as the 8% estimate does not take into account additional losses linked to droughts, floods and other extre -

me events, while policy responses to shocks (stockpiling by importers and increased export restrictions) may amplify and prolong price pres -
sures. The rice market between 2023 and 2024 illustrates this well, as the combination of rice-export restrictions imposed by India in July 2023
(in order to protect its domestic market) and of the drought in South-East Asia contributed to a 30% average increase in the price of rice over the
course of 2023 and kept global prices high for several months after the end of the El Niño episode.

Energy production sector: heavy reliance on hydroelectricity leaves the sector vulnerable to a second supply shock
Droughts will reduce hydroelectric power generation in Latin America and South-East Asia. As a result, some countries will have to turn to other,
more expensive solutions, such as fossil fuels, against a backdrop of already high oil and gas prices. This shift will increase global demand for
oil and put pressure on prices which, according to the IMF, could persist for up to four quarters after the initial El Niño shock. For example,

in Colombia, where hydropower accounts for 55% of electricity generation, a 30% drop in output (already seen in 2023–2024) could increase the
cost of electricity generation by 15 to 20% (assuming that the cost of thermal power generation is twice as high as hydropower). In Ecuador (where
hydropower accounts for 70% of electricity generation) and Vietnam (30%), electricity shortages are likely to disrupt local industries.
SOURCE: IMF, WORLD BANK, EMBER, BNP PARIBAS CALCULATIONS

Reading note: Scores are standardised on a scale of 0 to 10 for ease of comparison between countries and supply channels.
- Food vulnerability: based on the weight of food in the CPI.
- Energy vulnerability: based on the share of hydropower in electricity generation.
- Logistics vulnerability: the ‘Trade and transport-related infrastructure’ component of the World Bank’s Logistics Perfor-

mance Index, which measures the quality of the infrastructure required for transport and international trade.

Chart of the Week / 30 September 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
Logistics: transport infrastructure is another point of vulnerability
The final channel relates to disruptions to freight transport against a backdrop of already weakened value chains. The variable used in our
heatmap is a proxy for economies’ abilities to absorb a shock (particularly a climate-related one) affecting their transport infrastructure (for

example, by maintaining traffic flow, diverting or reorganising transport flows). The heatmap highlights the particularly high vulnerability
of Latin America. Its capacity to absorb a logistics shock is relatively limited, and there is significant exposure at a strategic chokepoint, the
Panama Canal, through which approximately 5% of global trade passes. Low rainfall in the canal basin has already led to the number of
daily transits being reduced in September 2026 (as in 2023–2024); further restrictions are expected in October (to 29 transits per day, down

from 36 in August), which will increase transport times and costs. Europe is also being affected, with low water levels on the Rhine and the
Danube already disrupting river transport and electricity generation.
Latin America and South-East Asia are hit hardest
Therefore, the economies most at risk are not necessarily those that will suffer the greatest climate shock, but those where multiple trans -
mission channels converge, including a high weighting of food prices in inflation, reliance on hydroelectricity, fragile transport infrastructure

and limited fiscal room for manoeuvre. Latin America and South-East Asia are home to some of the most vulnerable countries, particularly
due to their dependence on food prices and hydroelectricity. Colombia and Ecuador are noteworthy for their high exposure to energy risk,
while Ghana, India and the Philippines are some of the economies most vulnerable to a food shock. Latin America faces a combination of
vulnerabilities, such as high exposure to food and energy price shocks in some countries, which are compounded by logistics risks, as currently

illustrated by the restrictions on traffic through the Panama Canal.
helene.drouot@bnpparibas.com

Document 304: Global Strategy Views_ Competition for Capital

Source PDF: all report pdf/Global Strategy Views_ Competition for Capital.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-09-17
Pages: 12
Status: ok

### Page 1

1. There are two themes dominating our investor conversations: the impact of AI and 
the rise in interest rates. The two issues are linked as demand for capital from both 
the private and public sectors increase. In the private sector a surge in capex 
spending to fund AI infrastructure has eaten into free cash flow and forced 
companies to raise more in debt and equity markets. Meanwhile, government 
borrowing needs have increased as priorities shift towards upgrading critical 
infrastructure, energy security and defense at a time when cyclical inflationary 
pressures driven by higher energy prices are also resulting in higher policy rates. The 
combination has pushed up the cost of capital. As recently as 2022, for example, 
30-year bond yields in Germany and Japan were close to zero (Exhibit 1). A rise in 
yields, together with more uncertainty (over geopolitics and the future impact of AI) 
have, in combination, pushed up the cost of capital.

2. This rise in yields comes after a near-record period of equity outperformance 
relative to bonds over 10-year holding periods (Exhibit 2).

Exhibit 1: Rising yields and uncertainty have increased the cost of capital 
30-year government bond benchmark yields (%) 
          













### Page 2

As equities have continued to outperform bonds, equity risk premia have fallen back to 
levels last seen in the late 1990s, leaving equity markets more vulnerable to further 
increases in bond yields. That said, in the US and Japan, the ERP has bounced off recent 
lows (Exhibit 3).

3. The impact of rising bond yields, however, depends not only on the level of yields, but 
also on the reasons as well as the speed of the adjustment (Exhibit 4). It is the speed 
issue that has caused the biggest impact in the recent past. As our US strategists note, 
stocks have usually generated positive returns alongside rising interest rates unless the 
pace of rising rates exceeded 2+ standard deviations and this is a pattern that is broadly

the adjustments in recent days which explains the pullback in equity prices.

4. But despite the rise in yields, nominal GDP and profit growth remain strong and, until 
recently, the impact of rising bond yields has been offset by the strength in corporate 
profits. Earnings growth has been the main driver of equity returns over the past 18 
months in all regions (Exhibit 5). This strength has meant that PE multiples have either 
been flat (Japan and Europe) or have fallen (the US, Asia and EM).

### Page 4

the start of the year to 19x, in line with its long-run average, despite the market being 
close to its all-time high. 
5. While US equities have driven the bulk of equity outperformance for much of the 
fifteen years post the financial crisis, the improvement in earnings across regions, and 
the sharper de-rating in the US, has resulted in equity performance being 
broader-based by geography. Since 2025, the US equity market has been the weakest of 
the major regions (Exhibit 6), a turnaround from the dominant trend since the financial 
crisis and increasing investor returns from a more geographically diversified portfolio.

6. Supportive profit growth has been accompanied by positive earnings revisions, with 
2026 and 2027 estimates moving higher across major regions. There are four broad 
areas which have been driving much of this profit growth. First, technology earnings 
continue to be very robust. Second, rising energy prices have pushed up profits in the 
commodity sector. Third, banks have generally enjoyed strong earnings backed by 
positive nominal GDP growth, steep yield curves and strong private sector balance 
sheets. Finally, sectors such as Industrials have benefited from the surge in AI capex 
spending which has spilled over into improved revenues for the ‘pick and shovels’ of AI 
infrastructure. The breadth of the earnings’ growth has also increased the opportunity 
for investors to diversify across sectors as well as countries.

7. While technology profit growth has remained strong, the surge in capex spending 
among the hyperscalers has increasingly eaten through their free cash flow (Exhibit 8) 
prompting companies to look for alternative sources of funding, in the credit and equity 
markets.  The capex spending growth for AA-rated issuers has been substantial: the 65% 
year-over-year growth in Q2 marks the 10th consecutive quarter that aggregate AA 
capex growth exceeds 35%. They have also turned to the convertible bond market 
where volume has also increased year to date reaching $135 billion in the US, with 
AI-related borrowers driving 44% of total issuance, on our estimates. 
Our Credit team raised their full-year US IG gross issuance forecast by $200bn to $2.3 
trillion and AI-related issuers account for a quarter of USD IG gross supply this year, 
pushing overall supply to a year-to-date record.

The combination of higher cost of capital and greater capital intensity in the tech sector 
has reduced the value of their future cash flows and triggered a de-rating in the biggest 
companies.  The dominant capex hyperscalers in the US now have a forward PE close to 
the average of the rest of the market.

8. Software companies have also de-rated on the back of fears of disruption from AI 
while memory and chips stocks have also experienced lower multiples as their earnings

### Page 7

surge. Globally technology has seen its PE ratio moderate, falling below its 20-year 
median (Exhibit 10). Meanwhile, the spending of these companies has spilled over into 
better earnings and more visible cash flows in many other parts of the economy. 
Healthcare, Consumer Discretionary and Industrials are all more expensive in absolute 
terms than IT, and Industrials has re-rated and now has a PE close to the 90th percentile 
of its 20-year range.

9. For these reasons, we have argued that Technology equities have not experienced a 
classic bubble because valuation levels are much lower than in previous financial 
bubbles. Nevertheless, the risk for investors is less about valuation and more whether 
there is an unsustainable earnings bubble. Bubbles in earnings, rather than valuations, 
have occurred in previous periods.  The Bank sector, for example, briefly became the 
biggest sector in the S&P 500 in the run up to the financial crisis of 2008/09 (Exhibit 11). 
Unlike the technology bubble of the late 1990s, or the Japanese bubble of the late 
1980s, Bank stocks did not experience a major valuation bubble at the time, but the 
surge in earnings that drove the sector’s outperformance proved to be unsustainable.

### Page 8

There are, however, important differences to the Tech sector today. The earnings of the 
banks were boosted by sharply rising leverage and the asset that they were financing – 
US real estate – did experience a valuation bubble. When the housing market collapsed, 
pushing economies into recession, it triggered a sharp fall in underlying earnings for the 
banking sector. There are important differences today. First, technology profits remain 
very robust, and balance sheets are strong overall. Second, interest coverage ratios for 
the aggregate S&P 500 and its median stock, for example, rank in the 99th and 68th 
percentiles relative to the past 20 years. Third, demand for compute is accelerating and 
outstripping supply; Microsoft recently declared their intent to triple data centre 
capacity in 6 years. The bottlenecks are increasingly power, land and data centre 
capacity rather than a lack of compute demand. At our annual Communacopia 
Technology Conference, Nvidia reiterated its 2030 AI TAM outlook to $3tn-$4tn and 
supply constraints continue to be broad based. Nonetheless, any slowdown in profit 
growth, in an environment of a much higher cost of capital, could put downward 
pressure on equity prices, reducing confidence in future cash flows across the ecosystem 
from the hyperscalers to the ‘pick and shovels’ that have been benefiting from the capex 
boom.  
10. Given the near-term risks in bond markets, we remain neutral on equities, alongside 
other asset classes on a 3-month horizon, but continue to be overweight equities over 
12 months. While profit growth is likely to slow from the recent strong pace, ongoing 
growth in nominal GDP should underpin equity markets and the earnings cycle 
propelling equity returns even without any valuation expansion from current levels and 
our economists continue to see a low risk of recession. As our asset allocation team has 
shown, earnings growth and ROE are likely to continue to be the key drivers of equity 
performance relative to bonds, and we expect these fundamentals to remain supportive 
for equity returns. Nevertheless, we continue to recommend well diversified equity 
exposure as opportunities continue to broaden across geographies, sectors and factors.

Stock correlations are at multi-year lows across the major equity markets (Exhibit 12).

Even within sectors like technology, correlations have fallen sharply (Exhibit 13). The 
‘magnificent seven’ that was long seen as a homogeneous group of technology winners 
has also seen significant dispersion of returns. Differentiation, diversification and alpha 
generation are likely to remain the key drivers of equity returns. With higher bond yields, 
valuations are not likely to rise making earnings the critical driver of returns for investors.

Document 313: Irrational Exuberance In S&P 500 FEMO_

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Irrational Exuberance In S&P 500 FEMO?
Date:    6 hours ago
Irrational Exuberance In S&P 500 FEMO?

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Irrational Exuberance In S&P
500 FEMO?
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 21 Oct 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Chris Geirman / Unsplash
Are analysts' earnings expectations for the S&P 500 irrationally
exuberant? So far this year, FEMO (fabulous earnings momentum, as
measured by actual earnings per share) has been soaring, while FOMO
(fear of missing out, as reflected in P/E multiples) has been mostly
retreating. Much of that earnings optimism is concentrated in the S&P 500
Information Technology sector, particularly the Semiconductors industry,

### Page 3

where the AI investment boom has fueled extraordinary demand, pricing
power, and profit margins. But can these conditions persist? Let's take a
closer look:
(1) FEMO overwhelming FOMO. The S&P 500 is up 13.3% ytd, despite a
13.5% contraction in its forward P/E. That's because FEMO has
overwhelmed waning FOMO, with forward earnings jumping 31.0% since
the start of the year (chart).
The divergence is even more striking in Information Technology. The

sector's forward earnings has soared 65.5% ytd, while its forward P/E has
plunged 25.9%. The S&P 500 IT stock price index has risen 22.6% ytd,
powered by FEMO (chart).

### Page 4

Industry analysts remain remarkably bullish on the S&P 500's
fundamentals. Forward revenues is up 15.7% y/y, while forward earnings
is up 39.0% (chart).
(2) Semiconductors driving FEMO. Much of that optimism originates in
the IT sector, where forward revenues and earnings are up 57.2% and
91.4% y/y, respectively (chart).
Digging deeper reveals that the sector's extraordinary FEMO originates
largely in the Semiconductors industry, where forward revenues and

earnings are up 121.7% and 176.0% y/y, respectively (chart).

### Page 5

Indeed, semiconductors now account for a record 55.0% of the IT sector's
forward earnings and 40.9% of its market capitalization (chart).
The growing importance of semiconductors extends to the broader
market. Semiconductors now account for a record 20.4% of the S&P 500’s
forward earnings and 15.8% of its market capitalization (chart).

### Page 6

That makes the industry's earnings trajectory increasingly important for
the entire index. Analysts expect Semiconductor revenues and earnings to
grow 61.3% and 74.0% over the next year, respectively. More remarkably,
they expect earnings growth to average 66.1% annually over the next five
years.
These extraordinary expectations help explain why analysts project IT
sector earnings growth of 44.8% over the next year and 42.0% annually
over the next five years (chart).

In turn, analysts expect S&P 500 earnings to grow 19.0% over the next
year and an average of 26.1% annually over the next five years, the latter
approaching a record high.

### Page 7

(3) Pricing power fueling Semiconductors' FEMO. To determine
whether S&P 500 FEMO shows irrational exuberance, we need to assess
whether Semiconductor FEMO does so. Much of the industry's
extraordinary earnings growth reflects unprecedented profit margin
expansion. The industry’s forward profit margin nearly doubled from the
2023 lows to a record 51.8% during the week of October 20 (chart).
The explanation is straightforward: Demand for chips has surged as

hyperscalers and neocloud providers have raced to build AI infrastructure,
while supply is capacity constrained. The resulting pricing pressures are
clearly evident in the PPI for electronic components & accessories (up
26.8% y/y through September) and the import price index for computer &
electronic product manufacturing (up 12.4% y/y through September)
(chart).

### Page 8

(4) Will Semiconductors' pricing power persist? That's the critical
question for assessing whether Semiconductor FEMO is irrationally
exuberant. Investors already seem skeptical. The Semiconductor
industry's forward P/E has plunged to just 14.2 despite surging earnings
expectations (chart). In other words, unlike the tech bubble of 1999, any
irrational exuberance in FEMO has been mostly offset by depressed
FOMO!
The goods news is that demand for chips should remain strong. The nine

largest cloud service providers are expected to spend $957 billion on
capex this year, up 106% y/y, and more than $1.5 trillion in 2027. But
supply is starting to catch up. Global semiconductor manufacturing
capacity is expected to expand 5% this year and another 6% in 2027,
potentially easing shortages and price pressures.
(5) Irrational earnings exuberance? We suspect there is some irrational
exuberance in Semiconductor FEMO, particularly in analysts' consensus

long-term earnings growth expectations. Today's demand-supply
imbalance supports extraordinary profits, but sustaining current pricing
power and margins over a longer time horizon seems optimistic as
capacity expands. With Semiconductors accounting for more than one-
fifth of S&P 500 forward earnings, that exuberance is reflected in the
broader index.
Nevertheless, FEMO remains robust beyond Semiconductors. Analysts
expect Q3 earnings growth of 30.7% y/y, with positive growth across all 11

sectors (chart).

### Page 9

Our diffusion indexes also show that an increasing share of S&P 500
companies is experiencing rising forward revenues and earnings (chart).
Combined with expectations that semiconductor demand will continue to
outpace supply, at least over the near term, this suggests that FEMO
remains fundamentally rational.

Despite risk, agricultural export prices to remain elevated.
• Ongoing Middle East tensions are increasing volatility and raising energy, input
and shipping costs, while broadly supporting prices for agricultural products.

Impacts vary depending on idiosyncratic supply and demand conditions.
• Risks are exacerbated by the impact of a now confirmed super-sized El Niño,
which could slice off up to 1ppt of New Zealand’s GDP should on-farm
production of both milk and meat contract significantly.
• We have revised up our farmgate milk price forecast for this season by 25c to
$9.75/kg, with milk powder prices holding up better than we expected. El Niño
presents a clear upside risk to this, as it does not appear to have been reflected

in GDT auction prices to date.
• Meanwhile average New Zealand beef export prices should remain elevated over
the coming year, as lean trimming prices reverse recent declines. Sheepmeat
prices are set to ease on rising Australian supply, but remain at elevated levels.
• Kiwifruit and apple export prices are expected to remain elevated, supported
by strong demand for premium varieties, although increasing supply is likely to
keep prices largely flat rather than drive further gains.

• Export prices for New Zealand logs are set to remain broadly stable to slightly
firmer, supported by lower log supply and balanced Chinese inventories.

Source: Macrobond, Stats NZ, Westpac
%yr Index
La Niña
El Niño
Farming is well placed to navigate impacts of a strong El Niño.
• Scientists have now confirmed that the current El Niño event will be the

strongest on record. Temperatures in the Pacific Ocean are now 3.05C above the
average, exceeding the record set in 2015.
• A supersized El Niño increases the probability of drier weather in eastern regions
of New Zealand in coming months, with wetter conditions likelier in the west and
southern areas of the country.
• The potential impact on GDP could be significant. Our previous research has
shown a severe drought could slice off as much as 1ppt from economic growth.

• Much of that is will reflect lower agriculture production, particularly in dairy,
sheep and beef farms. Increased on-farm costs and processing disruptions are
also likely.
• A 1% loss in GDP is likely an upper end estimate of the El Niño impact. A key
consideration is soil moisture levels are currently high, implying some resilience
to future drier conditions.
• Farming practices have become more resilient to prolonged dry periods.
Irrigation is more widely used than in past dry periods and snowmelt levels

appear robust, which should be of particular benefit to Canterbury farms.

Conflict-related disruptions could push input costs higher than expected.
• Farm input costs are on the rise reflecting two key drivers: fuel and
fertiliser costs.
• Diesel and petrol prices have increased by about 50% compared to the same
period in 2025. While average prices fell in the September quarter, they closed

the quarter at an elevated level as tensions between the US and Iran increased
and broadened to encompass Saudi Arabia and the Yemeni Houthis.
• Fertiliser prices are also significantly higher. While the fertiliser market has
stabilised after a period of extreme volatility earlier this year, prices remain
highly sensitive to developments in the Middle East.
• Nitrogen (urea) prices have fallen substantially from a mid-year spike, with an
pick up in Chinese exports now boosting supply. These prices though remain

vulnerable to supply disruptions.
• By contrast, phosphate and other sulphur-based fertiliser prices are tracking
higher and remain at elevated levels, as supply constraints, trade restrictions
and disruptions to shipping offset the impact of softening demand in some
regions of the world.

forecasts
Farmgate milk price forecast revised up on strong protein demand.
• We have revised up our farmgate milk price forecast for this season by 25c to
$9.75/kg. This is above Fonterra’s forecast midpoint of $9.50/kg (range of $8.50-

10.50), but lower than futures market pricing of just over $10/kg.
• We have also opened our forecast for the 2027/28 season at $9.50/kg. Note that
there is a wide margin of uncertainty around the forecast at this stage, and we
will review as the next season approaches.
• Dairy prices at auction have generally held up better than our expectations,
although the performance has varied across products. Trends in global demand
are shifting away from fats and towards proteins, which has boosted demand

and prices for skim milk powder in particular.
• Our forecasts do not include an assumption about the impact of El Niño, which
presents a clear upside risk to prices and a downside risk to volumes. While the
futures market is pricing a strong lift in milk powder prices over the New Zealand
summer, there has been no apparent urgency to buy at the GDT auction.
Fonterra is forecasting a 2% lift in milk volumes over the rest of this season, on
top of what was already a strong increase last season.

• Global milk production also remains ample, although it has slowed in Europe
in recent months. El Niño is less likely to affect conditions among the major
producers in the Northern Hemisphere.

Source: AgriHQ, Stats NZ
Export beef prices to remain elevated over the next year.
• Beef export prices remain well supported but have softened in recent
months. The weakness has been concentrated in lean beef trimming prices,

which have eased since April as high US prices attracted additional imports,
particularly from South America, while tariff-free quotas temporarily boosted
product availability.
• In contrast, premium beef prices have remained firm, supported by tight global
supplies of high-quality beef, constrained US cattle numbers, and resilient
demand in higher-end retail and foodservice channels.
• We expect that lean trimming prices will reverse recent declines in coming

months as temporary increases in supply begin to fade and underlying
shortages, including the historically small US beef herd, re-emerge.
• In 2027, lean trimming prices should remain elevated but broadly flat as
strong US demand and a small US cattle herd are largely offset by rising global
production. From 2028 onwards, expanding global beef production gradually
eases market conditions, putting pressure on prices.
• Meanwhile, favourable supply/demand fundamentals to support premium beef

prices in 2027, with expanding global production to weigh on prices from 2028.
• Overall, beef export prices are likely to remain high, although both nominal and
real prices are expected to moderate over the medium term.

TWI weighted price
Lamb export prices to remain firm, but off recent highs.
• NZ sheepmeat export prices remain near record highs, supported by
constrained global supply and resilient demand from premium markets in

Europe, the UK and North America. Export values rose strongly through 2025/26
despite only modest growth in export volumes, highlighting the strength of
underlying prices.
• Global supply remains tight due to declining sheep flocks and lower slaughter
numbers in New Zealand, the UK and parts of Europe. However, rising Australian
production and exports are expected to gradually lift global supply and increase
competition in key export markets, limiting further price gains.

• Meanwhile, stronger demand from the UK, EU and North America has improved
export returns and reduced New Zealand's reliance on China, where economic
weakness has made consumers more price sensitive and encouraged a shift
towards cheaper proteins and cuts.
• The outlook remains positive but more balanced. Tight supply should keep
prices above historical averages over the coming year, although rising Australian
exports, softer demand growth, affordability pressures, signs of buyer

resistance and (eventually) a stronger NZ dollar could see prices ease modestly
from current elevated levels into 2027.

Capacity is catching up with demand, limiting further price gains.
• NZ kiwifruit export prices have pushed to record highs, supported by strong
demand in North America, Europe, Japan and Korea, as well as continued

consumer preference for premium varieties such as SunGold.
• Export prices continue to edge higher even as a record crop and larger fruit
volumes are helping to reduce market tightness. Zespri notes increased
competition from summer fruit, pricing pressure in parts of Asia and a larger-
than-expected crop.
• Kiwifruit export prices are set to remain high but mostly flat over the next
year, as strong demand for premium varieties such as SunGold and RubyRed is

increasingly offset by rising production and price pressures in Asia.
• Apple export prices are also at record highs, supported by strong demand in
Asia, North America and India, alongside a continued shift towards higher-value
cultivars and premium varieties.
• These factors should keep export prices elevated over the next 12 months,
supporting grower revenues and profitability.
• However, further gains could be limited. Rising New Zealand production,

recovering supplies from key growing regions and larger global apple crops are
expected to increase supply availability, reducing market tightness and keeping
prices broadly stable.

Source: AgriHQ
Index IndexLog prices look moribund.
• China remains the dominant market for New Zealand logs, with prices highly
sensitive to construction activity, sawmill demand and daily log offtake. In that
regard, weak housing and furniture demand continue to weigh on prices.

• Chinese softwood log inventories are estimated at around 2.2 to 2.5m m³,
equivalent to roughly 40 to 45 days of inventory cover at current offtake rates.
This suggests inventory levels are broadly balanced and exerting little pressure
on log prices.
• At the same time, reduced New Zealand log supply, including lower harvesting
volumes and the wind-down of salvage logging, is providing some support to
prices. Freight costs also remain an important driver of at-wharf-gate returns,

with currently high shipping costs crimping returns to forest owners.
• NZ log export prices are expected to remain broadly stable to modestly firmer
over the next 12 months, supported by lower domestic supply, balanced Chinese
inventory levels and tentative signs of stabilisation in log demand.
• However, any recovery is likely to be gradual, with weakness in China's property
sector, uncertainty around shipping costs and softer global growth continuing to

constrain demand and cap upside potential.

### Page 1

13030420     1

Transformation 
Quantum dictionary, part 1: Decoding the fundamentals 
21 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• What is quantum computing? How does it work? What is a qubit? And when will quantum reach its full potential? Quantum 
computing challenges our intuition and our understanding of what computers can do. In this two-part dictionary series, we 
decode key terms behind what could be the next major technological revolution. 
• Qubits are the basic building blocks of quantum computers, harnessing properties such as superposition and entanglement to 
process information. However, qubits are highly sensitive to their surroundings where disturbances can lead to errors in 
quantum computation, making error correction a key challenge for building quantum computers. 
• There is no single approach to building a quantum computer. Researchers and companies are pursuing a range of qubit 
technologies, from neutral atoms and photons to spin, superconducting, topological and trapped-ion qubits, while quantum 
computing itself is likely to increasingly work alongside classical computing and AI.

First, let’s lay the groundwork… 
Quantum computing  
Quantum computing uses qubits and quantum properties such as superposition (defined later on) to perform complex 
calculations and solve certain problems faster than classical computers.1 From a practical perspective, quantum computing could 
be used to tackle problems related to optimization, machine learning, simulation and factorization across a range of sectors, 
including autos and logistics, financial services, energy and climate, health and life sciences and national security. 
Current quantum computers can perform ‘just’ thousands of quantum operations. As the technology advances from thousands to 
millions, billions and eventually trillions of operations, quantum computers could begin to have commercial applications.  
• Millions: Financial services and security could experience some of the first use cases, such as threat detection, fraud 
prevention, derivatives pricing, bond trading and arbitrage. 
• Billions: Quantum could help power AI through advanced data generation capabilities.  
• Trillions: Quantum chemistry applications could emerge, including drug discovery, battery simulation and materials 
discovery. 
These are the publicly known applications, according to Oxford Quantum Circuits (OQC). And as quantum computing capabilities 
improve, some of these use cases could emerge sooner than anticipated.  
Now, we’ll tackle the core concepts… 
Qubits 
Qubits (quantum bits) are a quantum computer’s basic unit of information. They can exhibit quantum properties such as 
superposition and entanglement (which we elaborate on below) — properties fundamental to quantum computing. 
Quantum gates manipulate the state of qubits, much like logic gates manipulate bits in classical computers. Despite the name, a 
quantum gate is not a physical object, but rather an operation applied to a quantum system that changes its state. 
From physical to logical qubits 
Physical qubits are the foundation of a quantum computer’s hardware. However, they are highly susceptible to error. To address 
this, multiple physical qubits can be structured together to encode a logical qubit, allowing errors to be detected and corrected

1 Schneider, J., & Smalley, I. (2025, June 10). What is quantum computing? IBM. 
I NSTI TUTE
Accessible version

  I NSTI TUTE

and making the stored quantum information more reliable. In recent years, according to BofA Global Research, logical qubits 
have become a greater focus for quantum computing companies. 
Entanglement 
Entanglement is a condition where two or more qubits become linked so that their states are correlated, regardless of distance 
(Exhibit 1). It allows quantum computers to process information in ways that can be difficult or impossible for classical 
computers. 
So, how does it work? Suppose we have two photons that have opposite polarizations (the orientation of their light waves). 
Before measurement, neither has a definite polarization. When one is measured as horizontally polarized, the other will be 
vertically polarized when measured in the same basis, wherever it is. When measured separately, they appear to be random, but 
when compared, they are correlated (Exhibit 2). 
The challenges 
Once created, entanglement must be protected from decoherence (where interactions with the environment destroy quantum 
correlations). Maintaining entanglement over long distances is another challenge. Photons traveling through optical fiber can be 
absorbed or scattered, making long-distance quantum communication difficult. 2 Quantum repeaters aim to overcome this 
challenge by extending entanglement across a series of shorter links. 
Exhibit 1: Entanglement is a state where at least two qubits become 
correlated

determines the state of the other, no matter the distance 
An example of what entanglement means for qubit measurement

BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Superposition 
Unlike a classical bit, which can be one of two states (0 or 1), superposition allows a quantum system to exist in multiple states 
or configurations at the same time (Exhibit 3). For example, a qubit can exist in the 0 state, the 1 state or any state that is a 
combination of 0 and 1.3  
A quantum system can maintain a superposition until it is observed or measured. Once measured, its superposition collapses into 
a single state. Another way of thinking about superposition is to imagine a sphere with the north pole representing zero and the 
south pole representing one. Other points on the surface of the sphere represent different superposition states. This allows us 
to: 1) represent complex forms of information; and 2) simulate atoms in a computer. 
Schrödinger’s cat 
In 1935, physicist Erwin Schrödinger proposed a thought experiment to illustrate the strange implications of quantum 
mechanics. In this scenario, a cat, a radioactive substance that decays very slowly, and a Geiger counter are put together in a 
sealed box. According to quantum mechanics, the system can be described as a superposition in which the cat is both alive and 
dead until the box is opened. Once the box is opened and the system is observed, the superposition collapses into a single 
outcome.
